# Agent Flow Builder, Wave 1 (Draw) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Ship the Draw wave of the Agent Flow Builder: a Next.js app where Nick draws agent workflows on a React Flow canvas with 13 node kinds, saves them as committed JSON plus browser drafts, and shares them as locked view-mode pages on an unlisted Vercel preview.

**Architecture:** A single Next.js 16 App Router app. Committed flows in `flows/*.json` are validated by a zod schema and statically built into `/flow/[slug]`; a zustand store (with zundo undo history) holds the live React Flow graph and persists drafts to localStorage through one subscriber. Four Flow Kit primitives are vendored from `flow-kit-worktree@b414ac9` and extended with an optional port suffix; a node catalog drives the palette, the inspector forms, the ports, and the renderers. `while` is a React Flow group node containing its body, so the graph is always acyclic.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript · Next.js 16.2.9 · React 19.2.4 · Tailwind v4 · shadcn (style `base-nova`, neutral, Base UI) · `@xyflow/react` ^12.11 · zustand 5 + zundo · zod 4 · lucide-react · Vitest 4 + Testing Library (jsdom) · Playwright 1.60 (Chromium + WebKit) · pnpm · GitHub Actions · Vercel.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-12-agent-flow-builder-design.md` (read it first; this plan argues from it).

## Global Constraints

Copied from the spec. Every task's requirements include this section.

- Branch for all implementation work: `feat/wave-1-draw`, cut from `spec/agent-flow-builder`. Never push to `main`. Every deploy is an unlisted Vercel preview.
- Package manager pnpm. Node 24 in CI and on Vercel. Versions pinned exactly as the spec's Stack table: `next@16.2.9`, `react@19.2.4`, `react-dom@19.2.4`, `@xyflow/react@^12.11.0`, `@base-ui/react@^1.5.0`, `tailwindcss@^4`, `@tailwindcss/postcss@^4`, `lucide-react@^1.17.0`, `vitest@^4.1`, `@playwright/test@^1.60`, `typescript@^5`, `zustand@^5`, `zundo` latest, `zod@^4`.
- shadcn `components.json`: style `base-nova`, `baseColor` neutral, `cssVariables` true, aliases `@/components`, `@/lib/utils`, `@/components/ui`, `@/lib`, `@/hooks`, icon library lucide, `rsc` true, `tsx` true, tailwind css `app/globals.css`.
- Conventions: `"use client"` first line on every interactive file · files kebab-case, exports PascalCase · `data-slot="<part>"` on every rendered part · `className` passthrough via `cn()` from `@/lib/utils` · controlled components with `on*` callbacks · no data fetching and no storage access inside components (the store owns persistence) · tests colocated as `<file>.test.ts(x)` except e2e in `e2e/`.
- No raw colors in `components/**` (hex, `oklch(`, Tailwind palette classes). `pnpm check:tokens` enforces it over `components/**/*.tsx` excluding `components/ui/**` and `*.test.tsx`.
- Vendored Flow Kit files keep their content byte-identical to `flow-kit-worktree@b414ac9` except the three deviations the spec names (port suffix in `flow-types.ts`; `port` and `validate` props in `typed-handle.tsx`). Each vendored file starts with a provenance header comment naming the source path and commit.
- Node kinds, groups, data fields, ports, handle-id codec, Flow JSON schema, keyboard shortcuts, status badge states, and view-mode behavior are exactly as the spec's Wave 1 section defines them. No extra kinds, no extra fields.
- Commit after every task with a conventional-commit subject and a `Co-Authored-By:` trailer as the last line, naming the model that actually executes the task. The task steps show the form:
  `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`
- Prose in README and docs: no em dashes, no exclamation marks.

---

## Shared interfaces

Canonical names and signatures. Every task's Interfaces block copies from here verbatim; a task may add private helpers but may not rename or reshape anything below.

### `components/flow-kit/flow-types.ts` (vendored, extended)

```ts
export const FLOW_STATUSES = ["idle", "queued", "streaming", "done", "failed", "locked"] as const;
export type FlowStatus = (typeof FLOW_STATUSES)[number];
export interface HandleTypeDef { label: string; cssVar: `--flow-${string}` }
export function registerHandleType(key: string, def: Partial<HandleTypeDef> & { label: string }): void;
export const getHandleType: (key: string) => HandleTypeDef | undefined;
export const handleTypeKeys: () => string[];
export type HandleDir = "in" | "out";
/** `{nodeId}:{dataType}:{dir}` or, with a port, `{nodeId}:{dataType}:{dir}@{port}`. Port must match /^[a-z0-9-]+$/. */
export function handleId(nodeId: string, dataType: string, dir: HandleDir, port?: string): string;
export function parseHandleId(id: string | null | undefined): { nodeId: string; dataType: string; dir: HandleDir; port?: string } | null;
/** Unchanged semantics: source dir out, target dir in, same dataType. Ports are ignored here. */
export function isValidFlowConnection(c: { sourceHandle?: string | null; targetHandle?: string | null }): boolean;
export type NodeSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";
export const NODE_WIDTH: Record<NodeSize, number>;
```

### `components/flow-kit/typed-handle.tsx` (vendored, extended)

```ts
export interface TypedHandleProps extends Omit<HandleProps, "type" | "position" | "id" | "isValidConnection"> {
  nodeId?: string;
  dataType: string;
  type: "source" | "target";
  position?: Position;
  top?: number;
  className?: string;
  /** Optional port name, appended to the handle id as `@{port}`. */
  port?: string;
  /** Extra validator ANDed with the built-in isValidFlowConnection. */
  validate?: (c: Connection | Edge) => boolean;
}
export function TypedHandle(props: TypedHandleProps): JSX.Element;
```

### `components/flow-kit/typed-edge.tsx` (vendored, unchanged)

```ts
export function edgeColorFromHandle(sourceHandle?: string | null): string;
export function typedEdgeStyle(opts: { sourceHandle?: string | null; streaming?: boolean; selected?: boolean }): { style: React.CSSProperties; className: string };
export type TypedEdgeType = Edge<{ streaming?: boolean }, "typed">;
export function TypedEdge(props: EdgeProps<TypedEdgeType>): JSX.Element;
```

### `components/flow-kit/flow-tokens.css` (vendored, extended)

Keeps every existing token. Adds, with light values in `:root` and dark values in `.dark`:
`--flow-control` (light and dark: `var(--muted-foreground)`), `--flow-core`, `--flow-tools`, `--flow-logic`, `--flow-data`, `--flow-note-yellow`, `--flow-note-blue`, `--flow-note-green`, `--flow-note-pink`. New color values are `oklch(...)` literals inside this CSS file only (the token file is the single place raw colors are allowed).

### `lib/flow-schema.ts`

```ts
export const NODE_KINDS = ["start", "agent", "classify", "end", "note", "file-search", "guardrails", "mcp", "if-else", "while", "user-approval", "transform", "set-state"] as const;
export type NodeKind = (typeof NODE_KINDS)[number];
export type NodeGroup = "core" | "tools" | "logic" | "data";
export type FlowSource = "manual" | "claude-code" | "openclaw";

type Base = { label: string; description?: string };
export type StartData = Base & { trigger: "manual" | "schedule" | "webhook" | "event"; triggerDetail?: string };
export type AgentData = Base & { instructions: string; model?: string; tools?: string[] };
export type ClassifyData = Base & { categories: string[] };            // min 1
export type EndData = Base & { outputLabel?: string };
export type NoteData = Base & { text: string; color: "yellow" | "blue" | "green" | "pink" };
export type FileSearchData = Base & { store: string; topK?: number };
export type GuardrailsData = Base & { checks: string[] };
export type McpData = Base & { server: string; tools?: string[] };
export type IfElseData = Base & { conditions: { label: string; expression: string }[] };  // min 1
export type WhileData = Base & { condition: string; maxIterations?: number };
export type UserApprovalData = Base & { prompt: string };
export type TransformData = Base & { expression: string };
export type SetStateData = Base & { assignments: { key: string; value: string }[] };

export type KindData = {
  start: StartData; agent: AgentData; classify: ClassifyData; end: EndData; note: NoteData;
  "file-search": FileSearchData; guardrails: GuardrailsData; mcp: McpData; "if-else": IfElseData;
  while: WhileData; "user-approval": UserApprovalData; transform: TransformData; "set-state": SetStateData;
};
export type AnyKindData = KindData[NodeKind];

export type FlowNode<K extends NodeKind = NodeKind> = {
  id: string; kind: K; position: { x: number; y: number }; parentId?: string; width?: number; height?: number; data: KindData[K];
};
export type FlowEdge = { id: string; source: string; target: string; sourceHandle: string; targetHandle: string; label?: string };
export type FlowViewport = { x: number; y: number; zoom: number };
export type Flow = {
  schemaVersion: 1; id: string; name: string; description?: string; source: FlowSource; sourceRef?: string;
  createdAt: string; updatedAt: string; nodes: FlowNode[]; edges: FlowEdge[]; viewport?: FlowViewport;
};
export type FlowMeta = Pick<Flow, "id" | "name" | "description" | "source" | "sourceRef" | "createdAt" | "updatedAt">;

export const flowSchema: z.ZodType<Flow>;
export type ParseResult = { ok: true; flow: Flow } | { ok: false; errors: string[] };  // errors: "nodes[3].data.categories: expected at least 1 item"
export function parseFlow(json: unknown): ParseResult;      // runs migrateFlow first
export function migrateFlow(json: unknown): unknown;        // v1 passthrough; throws Error("unsupported schemaVersion X") otherwise
export function isSlug(s: string): boolean;                 // /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/
export function newDraftFlow(now?: Date): Flow;             // id `draft-` + 6 lowercase alphanumerics, name "Untitled flow", source "manual", one start node at {x:0,y:0}
```

Schema rules: `parentId` may only reference a node whose kind is `while` (refinement); every edge's `source`/`target` must exist (refinement); `sourceHandle`/`targetHandle` must parse with `parseHandleId` and carry the edge's `source`/`target` as `nodeId` (refinement); `id` must satisfy `isSlug`. Unknown `kind` fails with `nodes[i].kind: unknown kind "x"`.

### `lib/flow-handles.ts`

```ts
import { registerHandleType, handleId, type HandleDir } from "@/components/flow-kit/flow-types";
export const CONTROL = "control" as const;
registerHandleType(CONTROL, { label: "Control" });   // module scope, so importing this file is enough
export function portHandleId(nodeId: string, dir: HandleDir, port?: string): string;  // handleId(nodeId, CONTROL, dir, port)
```

### `lib/flow-convert.ts`

```ts
import type { Node, Edge } from "@xyflow/react";
export type BuilderNode = Node<AnyKindData, NodeKind>;                         // node.type is the kind
export type BuilderEdge = Edge<{ port?: string; streaming?: boolean }, "typed">;
export function toBuilderNode(n: FlowNode): BuilderNode;   // type=kind; extent "parent" when parentId; zIndex -1 for note; width/height copied when present
export function fromBuilderNode(n: BuilderNode): FlowNode; // drops selected, dragging, measured, extent, zIndex; keeps width/height only when defined
export function toBuilderEdge(e: FlowEdge): BuilderEdge;   // type "typed"; data.port from parseHandleId(sourceHandle)?.port
export function fromBuilderEdge(e: BuilderEdge): FlowEdge;
export function sortParentsFirst(nodes: BuilderNode[]): BuilderNode[];   // stable; React Flow requires parents before children
export function flowToGraph(flow: Flow): { nodes: BuilderNode[]; edges: BuilderEdge[] };
export function graphToFlow(meta: FlowMeta, nodes: BuilderNode[], edges: BuilderEdge[], viewport?: FlowViewport): Flow;  // updatedAt untouched; callers set it
export function canonicalFlowJson(flow: Flow): string;     // JSON with sorted keys at every level, 2-space indent, trailing newline
```

### `components/builder/node-catalog.ts`

```ts
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
export type PortSpec = { dir: HandleDir; port?: string; label?: string };
export type FieldDescriptor =
  | { type: "text"; key: string; label: string; placeholder?: string }
  | { type: "textarea"; key: string; label: string; placeholder?: string }
  | { type: "number"; key: string; label: string; min?: number }
  | { type: "select"; key: string; label: string; options: { value: string; label: string }[] }
  | { type: "chips"; key: string; label: string }             // string[]
  | { type: "string-list"; key: string; label: string; itemLabel: string; min?: number }   // string[] rows
  | { type: "condition-list"; key: "conditions"; label: string }                           // { label, expression }[]
  | { type: "kv-list"; key: "assignments"; label: string };                                // { key, value }[]
export type Renderer = "kind" | "terminal" | "branch" | "while" | "note";
export interface CatalogEntry<K extends NodeKind = NodeKind> {
  kind: K; label: string; group: NodeGroup; icon: LucideIcon; renderer: Renderer;
  subtitle?: (data: KindData[K]) => string | undefined;
  defaultData: () => KindData[K];
  ports: (data: KindData[K]) => PortSpec[];
  fields: FieldDescriptor[];
  defaultSize?: { width: number; height: number };   // note {220,140}, while {480,320}; others undefined
}
export const NODE_CATALOG: { [K in NodeKind]: CatalogEntry<K> };
export const PALETTE_GROUPS: { group: NodeGroup; label: string; kinds: NodeKind[] }[];   // Core, Tools, Logic, Data in spec order
export function catalogFor(kind: NodeKind): CatalogEntry;
export function portsFor(node: BuilderNode): PortSpec[];
export function portLabel(node: BuilderNode, port: string | undefined): string | undefined;
```

Ports per kind (exact port names): `start` → out · `agent`, `mcp`, `file-search`, `transform`, `set-state`, `while` → in, out · `end` → in · `note` → none · `classify` → in, then `cat-{i}` (label = category text) per category · `guardrails` → in, `pass` "Pass", `fail` "Fail" · `if-else` → in, then `c{i}` (label = condition label) per condition, then `else` "Else" · `user-approval` → in, `approve` "Approve", `reject` "Reject". Icons (lucide): start `Play`, agent `Bot`, classify `ListChecks`, end `Square`, note `StickyNote`, file-search `FileSearch`, guardrails `ShieldCheck`, mcp `Plug`, if-else `GitBranch`, while `Repeat`, user-approval `UserCheck`, transform `Shuffle`, set-state `Database`.

### `lib/connection-rules.ts`

```ts
import type { Connection, Edge } from "@xyflow/react";
/** isValidFlowConnection(c) && source !== target && parentId(source) === parentId(target). Unknown node ids -> false. */
export function isValidBuilderConnection(c: Connection | Edge, nodes: BuilderNode[]): boolean;
export function edgeIdFor(c: Connection): string;   // `${source}${port ? "@" + port : ""}->${target}`
```

### `lib/group-utils.ts`

```ts
import type { XYPosition } from "@xyflow/react";
export type Rect = { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number };
export function absolutePosition(node: BuilderNode, nodes: BuilderNode[]): XYPosition;   // sums parent chain
export function nodeBounds(node: BuilderNode, nodes: BuilderNode[]): Rect;  // width/height ?? measured ?? catalog defaultSize ?? {260, 80}
export function findWhileAt(point: XYPosition, nodes: BuilderNode[], excludeId?: string): BuilderNode | undefined;  // innermost while containing point
export function toRelativePosition(point: XYPosition, parent: BuilderNode, nodes: BuilderNode[]): XYPosition;
```

### `lib/storage.ts`

```ts
export const DRAFT_PREFIX = "sat:flow:";
export class StorageWriteError extends Error {}
export function draftKey(slug: string): string;
export function isStorageAvailable(): boolean;      // try/catch around a probe write
export function loadDraft(slug: string): Flow | null; // parseFlow; invalid or missing -> null
export function saveDraft(flow: Flow): void;         // throws StorageWriteError
export function deleteDraft(slug: string): void;
export function listDrafts(): Flow[];                // by updatedAt desc; skips unparsable entries
```

### `lib/flow-store.ts`

```ts
import type { StoreApi } from "zustand";
import type { TemporalState } from "zundo";
import type { Connection, EdgeChange, NodeChange, XYPosition } from "@xyflow/react";
export type Tool = "select" | "hand";
export type Mode = "edit" | "view";
export type SaveStatus = "saved" | "draft" | "unsaved-draft" | "not-saving";
export interface FlowState {
  meta: FlowMeta; nodes: BuilderNode[]; edges: BuilderEdge[]; viewport?: FlowViewport;
  mode: Mode; tool: Tool; storageStatus: "ok" | "failing";
  fileSnapshot: string | null;               // canonicalFlowJson of the committed file, null for browser-only drafts
  setMeta(patch: Partial<Pick<FlowMeta, "name" | "description">>): void;
  setMode(mode: Mode): void; setTool(tool: Tool): void; setStorageStatus(s: "ok" | "failing"): void;
  addNode(kind: NodeKind, position: XYPosition, parentId?: string): string;   // returns id `${kind}-${nanoid-like 6}`; nodes stay parent-first; new node selected, others deselected
  updateNodeData(id: string, patch: Partial<AnyKindData>): void;
  applyNodeChanges(changes: NodeChange<BuilderNode>[]): void;   // pauses temporal while any position change has dragging=true, resumes on the first with dragging=false
  applyEdgeChanges(changes: EdgeChange<BuilderEdge>[]): void;
  connect(c: Connection): void;              // no-op unless isValidBuilderConnection; edge {id: edgeIdFor(c), type:"typed", data:{port}}
  removeSelection(): void;                   // removes selected nodes, their descendants, and touching edges
  duplicateSelection(): void;                // clones selected nodes (+ descendants and internal edges) offset {x:40,y:40}, new ids, selects the clones
  setParent(id: string, parentId: string | undefined, position: XYPosition): void;  // position is relative to the new parent (or absolute when undefined)
  setViewport(v: FlowViewport): void;
  importFlow(flow: Flow): void;              // replaces meta, nodes, edges, viewport; clears temporal history
  exportFlow(): Flow;                        // graphToFlow(...) with updatedAt = new Date().toISOString()
  markFile(flow: Flow | null): void;         // sets fileSnapshot
}
export type FlowStore = StoreApi<FlowState> & { temporal: StoreApi<TemporalState<Partial<FlowState>>> };
export function createFlowStore(initial: Flow, opts?: { file?: Flow | null; mode?: Mode }): FlowStore;
export function saveStatus(s: FlowState): SaveStatus;   // not-saving if storageStatus failing; saved if fileSnapshot equals canonicalFlowJson(exportFlow() with updatedAt normalized); draft if fileSnapshot set; unsaved-draft otherwise
export const FlowStoreContext: React.Context<FlowStore | null>;
export function FlowStoreProvider(props: { store: FlowStore; children: React.ReactNode }): JSX.Element;
export function useFlowStore<T>(selector: (s: FlowState) => T): T;   // throws if used outside the provider
export function useFlowStoreApi(): FlowStore;
export function useFlowTemporal(): { undo: () => void; redo: () => void; canUndo: boolean; canRedo: boolean };
```

zundo options: `limit: 100`; `partialize` keeps `meta`, `nodes`, `edges` only; `equality` compares `canonicalFlowJson(graphToFlow(meta, nodes, edges))` of both states so selection, dragging and measurement never create entries.

### `lib/draft-persistence.ts`

```ts
export function bindDraftPersistence(store: FlowStore, slug: string, opts?: { debounceMs?: number }): () => void;
// subscribes to the store; 500 ms after the last graph or meta change writes saveDraft(exportFlow()) under `slug`; on StorageWriteError sets storageStatus "failing"; a later success sets "ok"; returns unsubscribe
```

### `lib/flows.server.ts` (server only)

```ts
export const FLOWS_DIR: string;                                   // path.join(process.cwd(), "flows")
export async function listFlows(dir?: string): Promise<Flow[]>;   // *.json sorted by name; throws Error(`${file}: ${errors.join("; ")}`) on invalid
export async function getFlow(slug: string, dir?: string): Promise<Flow | null>;
```

### Components (props)

```ts
// components/builder/flow-builder.tsx
export function FlowBuilder(props: { slug: string; file: Flow | null; initialMode: Mode }): JSX.Element;
// resolves initial flow = loadDraft(slug) ?? file; if both null renders <FlowNotFound slug/> (a link back to "/"); creates the store once (useState initializer), wraps in FlowStoreProvider, binds persistence in an effect (edit mode only), mounts useBuilderShortcuts, renders TopBar + NodePalette + FlowCanvas + CanvasToolbar + NodeInspector in edit mode and only FlowCanvas in view mode.

// components/builder/flow-canvas.tsx
export function FlowCanvas(props: { className?: string; children?: React.ReactNode }): JSX.Element;
// children render inside the ReactFlowProvider and after <ReactFlow>, so Task 11 can mount the palette, toolbar and inspector as overlays that still reach React Flow's hooks.
// reads nodes/edges/mode/tool from the store; nodeTypes from nodes/node-types.ts; edgeTypes { typed: BuilderEdge }; snapGrid [16,16]; Background dots; MiniMap; fitView in view mode; view mode sets nodesDraggable, nodesConnectable, elementsSelectable, deleteKeyCode=null to locked values; tool "hand" -> panOnDrag true and selectionOnDrag false, tool "select" -> panOnDrag [1,2] (middle and right mouse) and selectionOnDrag true; onDrop reads dataTransfer "application/sat-node-kind", converts with screenToFlowPosition, parents into findWhileAt(); onNodeDragStop parents a top-level node dropped over a while.

// components/builder/builder-edge.tsx
export function BuilderEdge(props: EdgeProps<BuilderEdge>): JSX.Element;   // TypedEdge + <EdgeLabelRenderer> pill with portLabel(sourceNode, data.port) when defined

// components/builder/node-palette.tsx
export function NodePalette(props: { className?: string }): JSX.Element;   // onClick -> addNode(kind, viewportCenterInFlowCoords); onDragStart sets dataTransfer "application/sat-node-kind" = kind

// components/builder/node-inspector.tsx
export function NodeInspector(props: { className?: string }): JSX.Element; // hidden when 0 selected; count + Delete when >1; fields from catalogFor(kind).fields when 1

// components/builder/top-bar.tsx
export function TopBar(props: { slug: string; file: Flow | null }): JSX.Element;

// components/builder/canvas-toolbar.tsx
export function CanvasToolbar(props: { className?: string }): JSX.Element;

// components/builder/flow-index.tsx
export function FlowIndex(props: { flows: Flow[] }): JSX.Element;

// components/builder/use-builder-shortcuts.ts
export function useBuilderShortcuts(): void;   // Delete/Backspace removeSelection · Mod+Z undo · Shift+Mod+Z redo · Mod+D duplicate · Escape deselect · V select tool · H hand tool; ignored while focus is in input, textarea, select, or contenteditable; no-op in view mode

// components/builder/use-connection-validator.ts
export function useConnectionValidator(): (c: Connection | Edge) => boolean;

// components/builder/nodes/node-types.ts
export const nodeTypes: Record<NodeKind, React.ComponentType<NodeProps<BuilderNode>>>;
```

Node renderer contract (all five): root element has `data-slot="node"` and `data-kind={kind}`; title in `data-slot="node-title"`; every port is a `TypedHandle` with `dataType={CONTROL}`, `port`, `validate={useConnectionValidator()}`, `aria-label={`${label ?? "Control"} ${dir === "in" ? "input" : "output"} port`}`; multiple out ports stack with `top` offsets computed from row index. `while-node` and `note-node` render `<NodeResizer minWidth minHeight isVisible={selected}>`; `while-node` body has `data-slot="while-body"` and the empty-state text "Drop nodes here" when it has no children.

## Shared DOM contract

Authoritative `data-slot` values. Unit tests and the Playwright journey select on these, so a task may add slots but may not rename one.

| Slot | Element |
| --- | --- |
| `node` | every node card root (all 13 kinds) |
| `node-header` · `node-title` | card header strip and its title text |
| `branch-row` | one row per out port on `if-else`, `classify`, `guardrails` |
| `while-body` | the `while` frame's drop region |
| `typed-handle` | every port (from the vendored `typed-handle`) |
| `edge-port-pill` | the port label pill on a typed edge |
| `flow-canvas` | the canvas wrapper, carrying `data-mode="edit" \| "view"` |
| `node-palette` · `palette-group` | the palette card and each group section |
| `canvas-toolbar` | the bottom tool pill |
| `top-bar` · `status-badge` | the top bar and its save-status badge |
| `inspector` · `import-errors` | the right panel and the import dialog's error list |
| `flow-builder` · `flow-not-found` · `edit-link` | the builder shell, its unknown-slug state, and view mode's way back to editing |
| `flow-index` · `flow-grid` · `flow-card` · `drafts-section` | the index page, its committed-flow grid and cards, and the browser-drafts section |

## Test conventions

- `vitest.config.ts`: `@vitejs/plugin-react`, `environment: "jsdom"`, `globals: true`, `setupFiles: ["./vitest.setup.ts"]`, `include: ["**/*.test.{ts,tsx}"]`, `exclude: ["e2e/**", "node_modules/**", ".next/**"]`, `css: false`, alias `@` → repo root.
- `vitest.setup.ts`: imports `@testing-library/jest-dom/vitest`; stubs `ResizeObserver`, `HTMLElement.prototype.scrollIntoView`, `hasPointerCapture`, `setPointerCapture`, `releasePointerCapture`, `window.matchMedia` exactly as the Flow Kit app does; adds a `localStorage` reset in `beforeEach`.
- Components that render handles or the canvas are wrapped in `<ReactFlowProvider>`; store-backed components are wrapped in `<FlowStoreProvider store={createFlowStore(fixtureFlow)}>`. A shared `lib/test-fixtures.ts` exports `fixtureFlow()` (start → agent → end, one note) and `renderWithStore(ui, store?)`.
- Canvas tests assert on DOM (`[data-id="..."]`, `[data-handleid="..."]`, `data-slot`), never on visibility or pixel positions. Interaction across the real canvas is Playwright's job.
- Store tests use `createFlowStore(fixtureFlow())` and call actions through `store.getState()`; undo through `store.temporal.getState().undo()`.
- Playwright: `playwright.config.ts` with projects `chromium` and `webkit`, `testDir: "./e2e"`, `baseURL: "http://127.0.0.1:3100"`, `webServer: { command: "pnpm start --port 3100", url, reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI }`. CI runs `pnpm build` before e2e.
- Commands: `pnpm test` (vitest run), `pnpm test -- <path>` for one file, `pnpm e2e` (playwright test), `pnpm lint`, `pnpm typecheck` (tsc --noEmit), `pnpm check:tokens`, `pnpm build`.

## Task index

Fourteen tasks. Several split into sub-tasks, each with its own test cycle and its own commit, where a reviewer could accept one half and reject the other. Execute in order: every task's Interfaces block consumes only what earlier tasks produced.

| # | Task | Sub-tasks | Deliverable |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Scaffold app, tooling, CI | | `pnpm lint typecheck check:tokens test build` green on an empty app; CI workflow |
| 2 | Vendor Flow Kit with port codec, `port`/`validate` props, tokens, diff script | | vendored tests plus new codec tests pass |
| 3 | Flow schema, `flow-handles`, `flow-convert`, `flows.server` | | parse, migrate, round-trip, build-time loading tested |
| 4 | Node catalog | | 13 entries; ports and defaults tested against the schema |
| 5 | Store, storage, draft persistence, connection rules | a connection rules · b storage · c store · d persistence | all store actions, undo coalescing, save status, debounced drafts tested |
| 6 | Group utils and `while-node` (drop-into-frame spike) | | absolute/relative math and frame rendering tested; spike findings recorded for Task 8 |
| 7 | Node renderers, `node-types`, `builder-edge` | a kind + terminal · b branch · c note + map · d edge | every kind renders its data and the right ports |
| 8 | Flow canvas | a core, tools, view lock · b drop and parenting | store-bound canvas |
| 9 | Palette, toolbar, top bar | a palette · b toolbar · c top bar core · d overflow menu | add, undo/redo, export/import/share/revert/delete wired |
| 10 | Inspector | a shell + simple fields · b list editors | catalog-driven forms |
| 11 | Pages, `flow-builder` shell, `flow-index`, shortcuts | a shortcuts · b shell · c index · d server pages | `/` and `/flow/[slug]` work end to end with drafts and unknown slugs |
| 12 | Seed flows from the three source skills | a ship · b new-project · c subagent-build-loop · d coverage test | three committed flows; the test proves all 13 kinds appear |
| 13 | Playwright journey and CI e2e | a config + journey · b CI job | the spec's journey green on Chromium and WebKit |
| 14 | Vercel preview and cross-browser verification | | unlisted preview URL verified in Chrome and Safari; README updated; PR opened |

- [ ] **Step 13: Write vitest.setup.ts**

Ported from the Flow Kit app's setup stubs (same jsdom shims: `ResizeObserver`, `scrollIntoView`, pointer capture, `matchMedia`), plus the `localStorage` reset this plan adds. The `window`/`localStorage` guards are necessary here, not decorative: Task 3's `flows.server.test.ts` runs under `// @vitest-environment node`, where `window` does not exist, and `setupFiles` still runs for every test file regardless of that file's own environment override.

```ts
import "@testing-library/jest-dom/vitest";
import { beforeEach } from "vitest";

// Base UI / floating-ui shims for jsdom (shadcn base-nova components use @base-ui/react)
class ResizeObserverStub {
  observe() {}
  unobserve() {}
  disconnect() {}
}
globalThis.ResizeObserver ??= ResizeObserverStub as unknown as typeof ResizeObserver;

// Guarded: this file also loads for node-environment test files (see lib/flows.server.test.ts
// in Task 3), where `window` does not exist.
if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
  window.HTMLElement.prototype.scrollIntoView ??= () => {};
  window.HTMLElement.prototype.hasPointerCapture ??= () => false;
  window.HTMLElement.prototype.setPointerCapture ??= () => {};
  window.HTMLElement.prototype.releasePointerCapture ??= () => {};
  window.matchMedia ??= ((query: string) => ({
    matches: false,
    media: query,
    onchange: null,
    addListener: () => {},
    removeListener: () => {},
    addEventListener: () => {},
    removeEventListener: () => {},
    dispatchEvent: () => false,
  })) as unknown as typeof window.matchMedia;
}

beforeEach(() => {
  if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") localStorage.clear();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 14: Write scripts/check-tokens.mjs**

Ported from the Flow Kit app's `scripts/check-tokens.mjs`: same three patterns (raw hex, raw `oklch()`, Tailwind palette utility classes) and the same exit behavior (exit 1 on any violation, exit 0 otherwise), with the glob changed to `components/**/*.tsx` and the exclusion changed to `components/ui/**` plus `.test.` files.

```js
import { globSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";

const FILES = globSync("components/**/*.tsx", {
  exclude: (f) => f.includes(".test.") || f.startsWith("components/ui/"),
});

// One entry per token-contract rule (design spec, Visual language section). Known limitation:
// issue refs like "#1234" in comments can false-positive as hex; use GH-1234 in component
// sources instead.
const PATTERNS = [
  { re: /#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}\b/g, why: "raw hex color" },
  { re: /\boklch\s*\(/gi, why: "raw oklch()" },
  {
    re: /\b(?:bg|text|border|ring|fill|stroke|from|via|to|outline|decoration|divide|accent|caret|shadow)-(?:slate|gray|zinc|neutral|stone|red|orange|amber|yellow|lime|green|emerald|teal|cyan|sky|blue|indigo|violet|purple|fuchsia|pink|rose)-\d{2,3}\b/g,
    why: "tailwind palette class",
  },
];

if (FILES.length === 0) {
  console.warn(
    "check:tokens: WARNING: no .tsx files found under components/. Gate has no coverage yet.",
  );
}

let violations = 0;
for (const file of FILES) {
  let source;
  try {
    source = readFileSync(file, "utf8");
  } catch (e) {
    violations++;
    console.error(`${file}: could not read (${e.code ?? e.message})`);
    continue;
  }
  source.split("\n").forEach((line, i) => {
    for (const { re, why } of PATTERNS) {
      if (re.test(line)) {
        violations++;
        console.error(`${file}:${i + 1}: ${why}: ${line.trim()}`);
      }
      re.lastIndex = 0;
    }
  });
}

if (violations) {
  console.error(`\ncheck:tokens: ${violations} violation(s). Use shadcn CSS variables.`);
  process.exit(1);
}
console.log(`check:tokens: ${FILES.length} file(s) clean.`);
```

- [ ] **Step 16: Write app/layout.tsx**

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import "./globals.css";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Agent Flow Builder",
  description: "Draw agent workflows on a node canvas.",
};

const THEME_SCRIPT = `
(function () {
  var mql = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
  var apply = function (matches) {
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle("dark", matches);
  };
  apply(mql.matches);
  mql.addEventListener("change", function (e) {
    apply(e.matches);
  });
})();
`;

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" suppressHydrationWarning>
      <head>
        <script dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: THEME_SCRIPT }} />
      </head>
      <body className="antialiased">{children}</body>
    </html>
  );
}
```

`suppressHydrationWarning` on `<html>` is required because the inline script mutates the `dark` class before React hydrates; without it React logs a hydration mismatch warning for an attribute it did not itself render. No `next-themes` dependency, per Global Constraints.

`lib/utils.ts` already exists from Step 6 (shadcn's generated `cn()`, built on `clsx` + `tailwind-merge`). Add the first test so `pnpm test` has something to run:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { cn } from "./utils";

describe("cn", () => {
  it("merges class names and resolves Tailwind conflicts", () => {
    expect(cn("px-2 py-1", "px-4")).toBe("py-1 px-4");
  });
  it("drops falsy values", () => {
    expect(cn("a", false, undefined, null, "b")).toBe("a b");
  });
});
```

### Task 2: Vendor Flow Kit with the port codec, `port`/`validate` props, tokens, diff script

**Files:**
- Create: `components/flow-kit/flow-types.ts`, `flow-types.test.ts`, `flow-tokens.css`, `typed-handle.tsx`, `typed-handle.test.tsx`, `typed-edge.tsx`, `typed-edge.test.tsx`, `scripts/flow-kit-diff.sh`
- Modify: `app/globals.css` (add the flow-tokens import), `package.json` (add `flow-kit:diff` script)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `cn()` from `lib/utils.ts` (Task 1).
- Produces (copied verbatim from the skeleton's Shared interfaces section - later tasks import these exact names):

```ts
// components/flow-kit/flow-types.ts (vendored, extended)
export const FLOW_STATUSES = ["idle", "queued", "streaming", "done", "failed", "locked"] as const;
export type FlowStatus = (typeof FLOW_STATUSES)[number];
export interface HandleTypeDef { label: string; cssVar: `--flow-${string}` }
export function registerHandleType(key: string, def: Partial<HandleTypeDef> & { label: string }): void;
export const getHandleType: (key: string) => HandleTypeDef | undefined;
export const handleTypeKeys: () => string[];
export type HandleDir = "in" | "out";
/** `{nodeId}:{dataType}:{dir}` or, with a port, `{nodeId}:{dataType}:{dir}@{port}`. Port must match /^[a-z0-9-]+$/. */
export function handleId(nodeId: string, dataType: string, dir: HandleDir, port?: string): string;
export function parseHandleId(id: string | null | undefined): { nodeId: string; dataType: string; dir: HandleDir; port?: string } | null;
/** Unchanged semantics: source dir out, target dir in, same dataType. Ports are ignored here. */
export function isValidFlowConnection(c: { sourceHandle?: string | null; targetHandle?: string | null }): boolean;
export type NodeSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";
export const NODE_WIDTH: Record<NodeSize, number>;
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the port suffix in flow-types.ts**

Replace the `handleId` / `parseHandleId` pair (lines below the `handleTypeKeys` export) with:

```ts
export type HandleDir = "in" | "out";

const PORT_RE = /^[a-z0-9-]+$/;

/** Handle id codec: `{nodeId}:{dataType}:{in|out}`, or with a port `{nodeId}:{dataType}:{in|out}@{port}`. */
export function handleId(nodeId: string, dataType: string, dir: HandleDir, port?: string): string {
  const base = `${nodeId}:${dataType}:${dir}`;
  if (port === undefined) return base;
  if (!PORT_RE.test(port)) {
    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production") {
      console.warn(`handleId: invalid port "${port}" - use lowercase letters, digits, hyphens; omitting suffix`);
    }
    return base;
  }
  return `${base}@${port}`;
}

export function parseHandleId(id: string | null | undefined) {
  if (!id) return null;
  const parts = id.split(":");
  if (parts.length < 3) return null;
  const dirSegment = parts.pop()!;
  const dataType = parts.pop()!;
  const nodeId = parts.join(":");
  const dirMatch = dirSegment.match(/^(in|out)(?:@(.+))?$/);
  if (!nodeId || !dataType || !dirMatch) return null;
  const dir = dirMatch[1] as HandleDir;
  const port = dirMatch[2];
  if (port !== undefined) {
    if (!PORT_RE.test(port)) return null;
    return { nodeId, dataType, dir, port } as { nodeId: string; dataType: string; dir: HandleDir; port: string };
  }
  return { nodeId, dataType, dir } as { nodeId: string; dataType: string; dir: HandleDir };
}
```

`isValidFlowConnection` is unchanged: it only reads `.dir` and `.dataType` off the parsed result, so a `port` field being present or absent never affects it - ports are ignored there by construction, matching the Shared interfaces contract.

- [ ] **Step 10: Implement port and validate in typed-handle.tsx**

Replace the whole file (provenance header from Step 2 stays on line 1):

```tsx
// Vendored from flow-kit-worktree apps/docs/registry/super-ai/flow/typed-handle.tsx @ b414ac9. Deviations: adds a port prop (feeds the handle id codec) and a validate prop (ANDed with isValidFlowConnection).
"use client";
import { Handle, Position, useNodeId, type Connection, type Edge, type HandleProps } from "@xyflow/react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { getHandleType, handleId, isValidFlowConnection } from "./flow-types";

export interface TypedHandleProps extends Omit<
  HandleProps,
  "type" | "position" | "id" | "isValidConnection"
> {
  /**
   * The React Flow node id that owns this handle.
   *
   * Optional — when rendered inside a React Flow node context the id is read
   * automatically via `useNodeId()`. Only pass this prop outside that context
   * (e.g. unit tests, Storybook, or standalone demos).
   */
  nodeId?: string;
  dataType: string;
  type: "source" | "target";
  position?: Position;
  /** vertical offset when stacking multiple ports on one side */
  top?: number;
  className?: string;
  /** Optional port name, appended to the handle id as `@{port}`. */
  port?: string;
  /** Extra validator ANDed with the built-in isValidFlowConnection. */
  validate?: (c: Connection | Edge) => boolean;
}

/**
 * A typed React Flow handle that encodes data-type semantics into the handle id
 * and enforces same-type, out→in connection validation.
 *
 * ### Handle id codec
 * Rendered handle ids follow the pattern `{nodeId}:{dataType}:{in|out}`, or
 * `{nodeId}:{dataType}:{in|out}@{port}` when a `port` prop is given.
 * This codec is consumed by typed-edge color derivation and connection
 * validation — do not bypass it by setting the `id` prop directly.
 *
 * ### Connection validation
 * Same-type, out to in connection validation is always applied and cannot be
 * bypassed. Pass `validate` to add extra rules; it is ANDed with the built-in
 * `isValidFlowConnection`, so a `validate` that returns `true` can never
 * approve a connection the built-in check rejects.
 *
 * ### Drag-state highlighting
 * `data-flow-compatible="true"` on the rendered handle is the extension hook
 * for drag-state highlighting (scale-up). Hosts can set it via
 * `useConnection()` from `@xyflow/react`. The companion "incompatible → dim"
 * treatment is deferred and recorded in the wave spec deviations.
 *
 * ### Custom type colors
 * Overriding `style.background` breaks type-color semantics. Register a custom
 * type via `registerHandleType` from `./flow-types` instead.
 */
export function TypedHandle({
  nodeId,
  dataType,
  type,
  position,
  top,
  className,
  style,
  port,
  validate,
  ...rest
}: TypedHandleProps) {
  const contextNodeId = useNodeId();
  const nid = nodeId ?? contextNodeId ?? "";
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production" && nid === "") {
    console.warn("TypedHandle: no nodeId available — handle id codec will be invalid");
  }
  const def = getHandleType(dataType);
  const dir = type === "source" ? "out" : "in";
  const isValidConnection = validate
    ? (c: Connection | Edge) => isValidFlowConnection(c) && validate(c)
    : isValidFlowConnection;
  return (
    <Handle
      id={handleId(nid, dataType, dir, port)}
      type={type}
      position={position ?? (type === "source" ? Position.Right : Position.Left)}
      isValidConnection={isValidConnection}
      aria-label={`${def?.label ?? dataType} ${dir === "in" ? "input" : "output"} port`}
      data-slot="typed-handle"
      data-flow-type={dataType}
      className={cn(
        "size-3.5 rounded-full border-2 border-background transition-transform",
        "data-[flow-compatible=true]:scale-125",
        className,
      )}
      style={{ background: `var(${def?.cssVar ?? "--flow-text"})`, ...(top != null && { top }), ...style }}
      {...rest}
    />
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 15: Write scripts/flow-kit-diff.sh**

Diffs each vendored file against its worktree source, stripping only the vendored copy's first (provenance) line before comparing - the worktree source has no such line, so only the vendored side is stripped. Informational: always exits 0.

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# scripts/flow-kit-diff.sh
# Compares each vendored components/flow-kit/ file against its source in flow-kit-worktree,
# ignoring the provenance header line this repo prepends to the vendored copy. Informational
# only: always exits 0, so it is safe to run in CI or locally without failing a build on
# upstream drift.
set -u

WORKTREE="${FLOW_KIT_WORKTREE:-/Users/nickv/ClaudeCode Projects/flow-kit-worktree}"
SRC_DIR="$WORKTREE/apps/docs/registry/super-ai/flow"
DST_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)/components/flow-kit"

FILES=(
  flow-types.ts
  flow-types.test.ts
  flow-tokens.css
  typed-handle.tsx
  typed-handle.test.tsx
  typed-edge.tsx
  typed-edge.test.tsx
)

drifted=0
for f in "${FILES[@]}"; do
  src="$SRC_DIR/$f"
  dst="$DST_DIR/$f"
  if [ ! -f "$src" ]; then
    echo "SKIP  $f (worktree source not found at $src)"
    continue
  fi
  if [ ! -f "$dst" ]; then
    echo "SKIP  $f (vendored file not found at $dst)"
    continue
  fi
  if diff -q "$src" <(tail -n +2 "$dst") >/dev/null; then
    echo "OK    $f"
  else
    drifted=$((drifted + 1))
    echo "DIFF  $f"
    diff -u "$src" <(tail -n +2 "$dst") | sed 's/^/      /'
  fi
done

echo ""
echo "flow-kit-diff: ${#FILES[@]} file(s) checked, $drifted with content drift beyond the header."
exit 0
```

```bash
chmod +x scripts/flow-kit-diff.sh
pnpm pkg set scripts."flow-kit:diff"="bash scripts/flow-kit-diff.sh"
pnpm run flow-kit:diff
```

Expected: exits 0; reports `DIFF` for `flow-types.ts`, `flow-types.test.ts`, `typed-handle.tsx`, `typed-handle.test.tsx` (the four files with deliberate content changes beyond the header) and `DIFF` for `flow-tokens.css` (the appended tokens), and `OK` for `typed-edge.tsx` and `typed-edge.test.tsx` (byte-identical beyond the header). This DIFF set is the expected baseline - a DIFF appearing on `typed-edge.tsx`/`typed-edge.test.tsx`, or a DIFF on the others that is not the documented change, means something drifted.

```ts
// lib/flow-handles.ts
import { registerHandleType, handleId, type HandleDir } from "@/components/flow-kit/flow-types";
export const CONTROL = "control" as const;
registerHandleType(CONTROL, { label: "Control" });   // module scope, so importing this file is enough
```

```ts
// lib/flow-schema.ts
export const NODE_KINDS = ["start", "agent", "classify", "end", "note", "file-search", "guardrails", "mcp", "if-else", "while", "user-approval", "transform", "set-state"] as const;
export type NodeKind = (typeof NODE_KINDS)[number];
export type NodeGroup = "core" | "tools" | "logic" | "data";
export type FlowSource = "manual" | "claude-code" | "openclaw";

type Base = { label: string; description?: string };
export type StartData = Base & { trigger: "manual" | "schedule" | "webhook" | "event"; triggerDetail?: string };
export type AgentData = Base & { instructions: string; model?: string; tools?: string[] };
export type ClassifyData = Base & { categories: string[] };            // min 1
export type EndData = Base & { outputLabel?: string };
export type NoteData = Base & { text: string; color: "yellow" | "blue" | "green" | "pink" };
export type FileSearchData = Base & { store: string; topK?: number };
export type GuardrailsData = Base & { checks: string[] };
export type McpData = Base & { server: string; tools?: string[] };
export type IfElseData = Base & { conditions: { label: string; expression: string }[] };  // min 1
export type WhileData = Base & { condition: string; maxIterations?: number };
export type UserApprovalData = Base & { prompt: string };
export type TransformData = Base & { expression: string };
export type SetStateData = Base & { assignments: { key: string; value: string }[] };

export type KindData = {
  start: StartData; agent: AgentData; classify: ClassifyData; end: EndData; note: NoteData;
  "file-search": FileSearchData; guardrails: GuardrailsData; mcp: McpData; "if-else": IfElseData;
  while: WhileData; "user-approval": UserApprovalData; transform: TransformData; "set-state": SetStateData;
};
export type AnyKindData = KindData[NodeKind];

export type FlowNode<K extends NodeKind = NodeKind> = {
  id: string; kind: K; position: { x: number; y: number }; parentId?: string; width?: number; height?: number; data: KindData[K];
};
export type FlowEdge = { id: string; source: string; target: string; sourceHandle: string; targetHandle: string; label?: string };
export type FlowViewport = { x: number; y: number; zoom: number };
export type Flow = {
  schemaVersion: 1; id: string; name: string; description?: string; source: FlowSource; sourceRef?: string;
  createdAt: string; updatedAt: string; nodes: FlowNode[]; edges: FlowEdge[]; viewport?: FlowViewport;
};
export type FlowMeta = Pick<Flow, "id" | "name" | "description" | "source" | "sourceRef" | "createdAt" | "updatedAt">;

export const flowSchema: z.ZodType<Flow>;
export type ParseResult = { ok: true; flow: Flow } | { ok: false; errors: string[] };  // errors: "nodes[3].data.categories: expected at least 1 item"
export function parseFlow(json: unknown): ParseResult;      // runs migrateFlow first
export function migrateFlow(json: unknown): unknown;        // v1 passthrough; throws Error("unsupported schemaVersion X") otherwise
export function isSlug(s: string): boolean;                 // /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/
export function newDraftFlow(now?: Date): Flow;             // id `draft-` + 6 lowercase alphanumerics, name "Untitled flow", source "manual", one start node at {x:0,y:0}
```

```ts
// lib/flow-convert.ts
import type { Node, Edge } from "@xyflow/react";
export type BuilderNode = Node<AnyKindData, NodeKind>;                         // node.type is the kind
export type BuilderEdge = Edge<{ port?: string; streaming?: boolean }, "typed">;
export function toBuilderNode(n: FlowNode): BuilderNode;   // type=kind; extent "parent" when parentId; zIndex -1 for note; width/height copied when present
export function fromBuilderNode(n: BuilderNode): FlowNode; // drops selected, dragging, measured, extent, zIndex; keeps width/height only when defined
export function toBuilderEdge(e: FlowEdge): BuilderEdge;   // type "typed"; data.port from parseHandleId(sourceHandle)?.port
export function fromBuilderEdge(e: BuilderEdge): FlowEdge;
export function sortParentsFirst(nodes: BuilderNode[]): BuilderNode[];   // stable; React Flow requires parents before children
export function flowToGraph(flow: Flow): { nodes: BuilderNode[]; edges: BuilderEdge[] };
export function graphToFlow(meta: FlowMeta, nodes: BuilderNode[], edges: BuilderEdge[], viewport?: FlowViewport): Flow;  // updatedAt untouched; callers set it
export function canonicalFlowJson(flow: Flow): string;     // JSON with sorted keys at every level, 2-space indent, trailing newline
```

```ts
// lib/flow-handles.ts
import { registerHandleType, handleId, type HandleDir } from "@/components/flow-kit/flow-types";

export const CONTROL = "control" as const;

registerHandleType(CONTROL, { label: "Control" });

export function portHandleId(nodeId: string, dir: HandleDir, port?: string): string {
  return handleId(nodeId, CONTROL, dir, port);
}
```

```ts
// lib/flow-schema.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { portHandleId } from "./flow-handles";
import {
  isSlug,
  migrateFlow,
  newDraftFlow,
  parseFlow,
  type Flow,
} from "./flow-schema";

function validFlow(): Flow {
  return {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    id: "valid-flow",
    name: "Valid flow",
    source: "manual",
    createdAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
    updatedAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
    nodes: [
      { id: "start-1", kind: "start", position: { x: 0, y: 0 }, data: { label: "Start", trigger: "manual" } },
      { id: "end-1", kind: "end", position: { x: 200, y: 0 }, data: { label: "End" } },
    ],
    edges: [
      {
        id: "start-1->end-1",
        source: "start-1",
        target: "end-1",
        sourceHandle: portHandleId("start-1", "out"),
        targetHandle: portHandleId("end-1", "in"),
      },
    ],
  };
}

describe("parseFlow", () => {
  it("parses a valid flow", () => {
    const result = parseFlow(validFlow());
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it("fails on an unknown node kind with the exact message", () => {
    const flow = validFlow();
    (flow.nodes[0] as unknown as { kind: string }).kind = "bogus";
    const result = parseFlow(flow);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!result.ok) expect(result.errors).toContain('nodes[0].kind: unknown kind "bogus"');
  });

  it("fails a classify node with zero categories", () => {
    const flow = validFlow();
    flow.nodes.push({
      id: "classify-1",
      kind: "classify",
      position: { x: 400, y: 0 },
      data: { label: "Classify", categories: [] },
    });
    const result = parseFlow(flow);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!result.ok) {
      expect(result.errors.some((e) => e.startsWith("nodes[2].data.categories:"))).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("fails when parentId points at an agent instead of a while node", () => {
    const flow = validFlow();
    flow.nodes.push({
      id: "agent-1",
      kind: "agent",
      position: { x: 400, y: 0 },
      data: { label: "Agent", instructions: "x" },
    });
    flow.nodes.push({
      id: "transform-1",
      kind: "transform",
      position: { x: 400, y: 160 },
      parentId: "agent-1",
      data: { label: "Transform", expression: "x" },
    });
    const result = parseFlow(flow);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!result.ok) {
      expect(result.errors.some((e) => e.includes('parentId "agent-1" does not reference a "while" node'))).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("fails when an edge targets a missing node", () => {
    const flow = validFlow();
    flow.edges.push({
      id: "end-1->ghost",
      source: "end-1",
      target: "ghost",
      sourceHandle: portHandleId("end-1", "out"),
      targetHandle: portHandleId("ghost", "in"),
    });
    const result = parseFlow(flow);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!result.ok) {
      expect(result.errors.some((e) => e.includes('target "ghost" does not reference an existing node'))).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("fails when a handle id does not match its edge endpoint", () => {
    const flow = validFlow();
    flow.edges[0].sourceHandle = portHandleId("end-1", "out"); // wrong node id encoded
    const result = parseFlow(flow);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!result.ok) {
      expect(result.errors.some((e) => e.startsWith("edges[0].sourceHandle:"))).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("round-trips through JSON unchanged", () => {
    const input = validFlow();
    const result = parseFlow(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(input)));
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (result.ok) expect(result.flow).toEqual(input);
  });
});

describe("migrateFlow", () => {
  it("passes version 1 through unchanged", () => {
    const flow = validFlow();
    expect(migrateFlow(flow)).toEqual(flow);
  });
  it("throws on an unsupported schemaVersion", () => {
    expect(() => migrateFlow({ schemaVersion: 2 })).toThrow("unsupported schemaVersion 2");
  });
});

describe("isSlug", () => {
  it("accepts lowercase alphanumeric-and-hyphen strings starting with a letter or digit", () => {
    expect(isSlug("ship")).toBe(true);
    expect(isSlug("draft-ab12cd")).toBe(true);
  });
  it("rejects empty strings, leading hyphens and uppercase", () => {
    expect(isSlug("")).toBe(false);
    expect(isSlug("-ship")).toBe(false);
    expect(isSlug("Ship")).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("newDraftFlow", () => {
  it("creates a slugged draft id that the schema accepts", () => {
    const flow = newDraftFlow(new Date("2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z"));
    expect(flow.id).toMatch(/^draft-[a-z0-9]{6}$/);
    const result = parseFlow(flow);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement flow-schema.ts**

```ts
// lib/flow-schema.ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { parseHandleId } from "@/components/flow-kit/flow-types";

export const NODE_KINDS = [
  "start",
  "agent",
  "classify",
  "end",
  "note",
  "file-search",
  "guardrails",
  "mcp",
  "if-else",
  "while",
  "user-approval",
  "transform",
  "set-state",
] as const;
export type NodeKind = (typeof NODE_KINDS)[number];
export type NodeGroup = "core" | "tools" | "logic" | "data";
export type FlowSource = "manual" | "claude-code" | "openclaw";

type Base = { label: string; description?: string };
export type StartData = Base & { trigger: "manual" | "schedule" | "webhook" | "event"; triggerDetail?: string };
export type AgentData = Base & { instructions: string; model?: string; tools?: string[] };
export type ClassifyData = Base & { categories: string[] };
export type EndData = Base & { outputLabel?: string };
export type NoteData = Base & { text: string; color: "yellow" | "blue" | "green" | "pink" };
export type FileSearchData = Base & { store: string; topK?: number };
export type GuardrailsData = Base & { checks: string[] };
export type McpData = Base & { server: string; tools?: string[] };
export type IfElseData = Base & { conditions: { label: string; expression: string }[] };
export type WhileData = Base & { condition: string; maxIterations?: number };
export type UserApprovalData = Base & { prompt: string };
export type TransformData = Base & { expression: string };
export type SetStateData = Base & { assignments: { key: string; value: string }[] };

export type KindData = {
  start: StartData; agent: AgentData; classify: ClassifyData; end: EndData; note: NoteData;
  "file-search": FileSearchData; guardrails: GuardrailsData; mcp: McpData; "if-else": IfElseData;
  while: WhileData; "user-approval": UserApprovalData; transform: TransformData; "set-state": SetStateData;
};
export type AnyKindData = KindData[NodeKind];

export type FlowNode<K extends NodeKind = NodeKind> = {
  id: string; kind: K; position: { x: number; y: number }; parentId?: string; width?: number; height?: number; data: KindData[K];
};
export type FlowEdge = { id: string; source: string; target: string; sourceHandle: string; targetHandle: string; label?: string };
export type FlowViewport = { x: number; y: number; zoom: number };
export type Flow = {
  schemaVersion: 1; id: string; name: string; description?: string; source: FlowSource; sourceRef?: string;
  createdAt: string; updatedAt: string; nodes: FlowNode[]; edges: FlowEdge[]; viewport?: FlowViewport;
};
export type FlowMeta = Pick<Flow, "id" | "name" | "description" | "source" | "sourceRef" | "createdAt" | "updatedAt">;

// ---- runtime validation ----

const positionSchema = z.object({ x: z.number(), y: z.number() });
const baseDataSchema = { label: z.string(), description: z.string().optional() };

const dataSchemas = {
  start: z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    trigger: z.enum(["manual", "schedule", "webhook", "event"]),
    triggerDetail: z.string().optional(),
  }),
  agent: z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    instructions: z.string(),
    model: z.string().optional(),
    tools: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
  }),
  classify: z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    categories: z.array(z.string()).min(1),
  }),
  end: z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    outputLabel: z.string().optional(),
  }),
  note: z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    text: z.string(),
    color: z.enum(["yellow", "blue", "green", "pink"]),
  }),
  "file-search": z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    store: z.string(),
    topK: z.number().optional(),
  }),
  guardrails: z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    checks: z.array(z.string()),
  }),
  mcp: z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    server: z.string(),
    tools: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
  }),
  "if-else": z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    conditions: z.array(z.object({ label: z.string(), expression: z.string() })).min(1),
  }),
  while: z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    condition: z.string(),
    maxIterations: z.number().optional(),
  }),
  "user-approval": z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    prompt: z.string(),
  }),
  transform: z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    expression: z.string(),
  }),
  "set-state": z.object({
    ...baseDataSchema,
    assignments: z.array(z.object({ key: z.string(), value: z.string() })),
  }),
} as const satisfies Record<NodeKind, z.ZodTypeAny>;

// Loose node/flow shapes: `kind` and `data` are validated by hand in the superRefine below so
// error messages and paths are fully controlled (an unknown kind must fail with the exact
// message `nodes[i].kind: unknown kind "x"`, which zod's built-in discriminated-union error
// does not produce verbatim).
const looseNodeSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  kind: z.string(),
  position: positionSchema,
  parentId: z.string().optional(),
  width: z.number().optional(),
  height: z.number().optional(),
  data: z.record(z.string(), z.unknown()),
});

const edgeSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  source: z.string(),
  target: z.string(),
  sourceHandle: z.string(),
  targetHandle: z.string(),
  label: z.string().optional(),
});

const viewportSchema = z.object({ x: z.number(), y: z.number(), zoom: z.number() });

export function isSlug(s: string): boolean {
  return /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/.test(s);
}

const looseFlowSchema = z.object({
  schemaVersion: z.literal(1),
  id: z.string(),
  name: z.string(),
  description: z.string().optional(),
  source: z.enum(["manual", "claude-code", "openclaw"]),
  sourceRef: z.string().optional(),
  createdAt: z.string(),
  updatedAt: z.string(),
  nodes: z.array(looseNodeSchema),
  edges: z.array(edgeSchema),
  viewport: viewportSchema.optional(),
});

export const flowSchema: z.ZodType<Flow> = looseFlowSchema.superRefine((flow, ctx) => {
  if (!isSlug(flow.id)) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: ["id"], message: "id must be a slug ([a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*)" });
  }

  const nodeIds = new Set(flow.nodes.map((n) => n.id));
  const whileIds = new Set(flow.nodes.filter((n) => n.kind === "while").map((n) => n.id));

  flow.nodes.forEach((node, i) => {
    if (!(NODE_KINDS as readonly string[]).includes(node.kind)) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: ["nodes", i, "kind"], message: `unknown kind "${node.kind}"` });
      return; // data shape is unknowable without a valid kind
    }
    const kind = node.kind as NodeKind;
    const dataResult = dataSchemas[kind].safeParse(node.data);
    if (!dataResult.success) {
      for (const issue of dataResult.error.issues) {
        ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: ["nodes", i, "data", ...issue.path], message: issue.message });
      }
    }
    if (node.parentId !== undefined && !whileIds.has(node.parentId)) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: "custom",
        path: ["nodes", i, "parentId"],
        message: `parentId "${node.parentId}" does not reference a "while" node`,
      });
    }
  });

  flow.edges.forEach((edge, i) => {
    if (!nodeIds.has(edge.source)) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: ["edges", i, "source"], message: `source "${edge.source}" does not reference an existing node` });
    }
    if (!nodeIds.has(edge.target)) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: ["edges", i, "target"], message: `target "${edge.target}" does not reference an existing node` });
    }
    const sourceParsed = parseHandleId(edge.sourceHandle);
    if (!sourceParsed || sourceParsed.nodeId !== edge.source) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: "custom",
        path: ["edges", i, "sourceHandle"],
        message: `sourceHandle "${edge.sourceHandle}" does not match source "${edge.source}"`,
      });
    }
    const targetParsed = parseHandleId(edge.targetHandle);
    if (!targetParsed || targetParsed.nodeId !== edge.target) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: "custom",
        path: ["edges", i, "targetHandle"],
        message: `targetHandle "${edge.targetHandle}" does not match target "${edge.target}"`,
      });
    }
  });
}) as unknown as z.ZodType<Flow>;

export type ParseResult = { ok: true; flow: Flow } | { ok: false; errors: string[] };

function formatPath(path: (string | number)[]): string {
  let out = "";
  for (const seg of path) {
    if (typeof seg === "number") out += `[${seg}]`;
    else out += out ? `.${seg}` : seg;
  }
  return out;
}

export function parseFlow(json: unknown): ParseResult {
  const migrated = migrateFlow(json);
  const result = flowSchema.safeParse(migrated);
  if (result.success) return { ok: true, flow: result.data as Flow };
  const errors = result.error.issues.map((issue) => {
    const path = formatPath(issue.path as (string | number)[]);
    return path ? `${path}: ${issue.message}` : issue.message;
  });
  return { ok: false, errors };
}

export function migrateFlow(json: unknown): unknown {
  const version = (json as { schemaVersion?: unknown } | null)?.schemaVersion;
  if (version === 1 || version === undefined) return json;
  throw new Error(`unsupported schemaVersion ${String(version)}`);
}

function randomSlug(length: number): string {
  const chars = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789";
  let out = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) out += chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];
  return out;
}

export function newDraftFlow(now: Date = new Date()): Flow {
  const iso = now.toISOString();
  return {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    id: `draft-${randomSlug(6)}`,
    name: "Untitled flow",
    source: "manual",
    createdAt: iso,
    updatedAt: iso,
    nodes: [
      { id: "start-1", kind: "start", position: { x: 0, y: 0 }, data: { label: "Start", trigger: "manual" } },
    ],
    edges: [],
  };
}
```

```ts
// lib/flow-convert.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { FlowMeta } from "./flow-schema";
import { fixtureFlow } from "./test-fixtures";
import {
  canonicalFlowJson,
  flowToGraph,
  fromBuilderEdge,
  fromBuilderNode,
  graphToFlow,
  sortParentsFirst,
  toBuilderEdge,
  toBuilderNode,
  type BuilderNode,
} from "./flow-convert";

describe("toBuilderNode", () => {
  it("sets type to the node kind", () => {
    const node = toBuilderNode({ id: "a", kind: "agent", position: { x: 0, y: 0 }, data: { label: "A", instructions: "x" } });
    expect(node.type).toBe("agent");
  });
  it("sets extent 'parent' when parentId is present", () => {
    const node = toBuilderNode({
      id: "a",
      kind: "agent",
      position: { x: 0, y: 0 },
      parentId: "while-1",
      data: { label: "A", instructions: "x" },
    });
    expect(node.extent).toBe("parent");
    expect(node.parentId).toBe("while-1");
  });
  it("sets zIndex -1 for note nodes only", () => {
    const note = toBuilderNode({ id: "n", kind: "note", position: { x: 0, y: 0 }, data: { label: "N", text: "x", color: "yellow" } });
    const agent = toBuilderNode({ id: "a", kind: "agent", position: { x: 0, y: 0 }, data: { label: "A", instructions: "x" } });
    expect(note.zIndex).toBe(-1);
    expect(agent.zIndex).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe("fromBuilderNode", () => {
  it("strips React Flow runtime fields", () => {
    const runtime = {
      id: "a",
      type: "agent",
      position: { x: 0, y: 0 },
      data: { label: "A", instructions: "x" },
      selected: true,
      dragging: true,
      measured: { width: 260, height: 80 },
      extent: "parent",
      zIndex: -1,
    } as unknown as BuilderNode;
    const flowNode = fromBuilderNode(runtime);
    expect(flowNode).not.toHaveProperty("selected");
    expect(flowNode).not.toHaveProperty("dragging");
    expect(flowNode).not.toHaveProperty("measured");
    expect(flowNode).not.toHaveProperty("extent");
    expect(flowNode).not.toHaveProperty("zIndex");
  });
});

describe("edge conversion round trip", () => {
  it("keeps sourceHandle/targetHandle and derives data.port", () => {
    const flowEdge = {
      id: "e1",
      source: "n1",
      target: "n2",
      sourceHandle: "n1:control:out@c0",
      targetHandle: "n2:control:in",
    };
    const builderEdge = toBuilderEdge(flowEdge);
    expect(builderEdge.sourceHandle).toBe("n1:control:out@c0");
    expect(builderEdge.targetHandle).toBe("n2:control:in");
    expect(builderEdge.data?.port).toBe("c0");
    expect(fromBuilderEdge(builderEdge)).toEqual(flowEdge);
  });
});

describe("flowToGraph / graphToFlow round trip", () => {
  it("produces canonical JSON equal to the canonical JSON of the input", () => {
    const input = fixtureFlow();
    const { nodes, edges } = flowToGraph(input);
    const meta: FlowMeta = {
      id: input.id,
      name: input.name,
      ...(input.description !== undefined && { description: input.description }),
      source: input.source,
      ...(input.sourceRef !== undefined && { sourceRef: input.sourceRef }),
      createdAt: input.createdAt,
      updatedAt: input.updatedAt,
    };
    const output = graphToFlow(meta, nodes, edges, input.viewport);
    expect(canonicalFlowJson(output)).toBe(canonicalFlowJson(input));
  });
});

describe("sortParentsFirst", () => {
  it("moves a parent before its child regardless of input order", () => {
    const child: BuilderNode = {
      id: "child",
      type: "agent",
      position: { x: 0, y: 0 },
      parentId: "parent",
      data: { label: "Child", instructions: "x" },
    };
    const parent: BuilderNode = {
      id: "parent",
      type: "while",
      position: { x: 0, y: 0 },
      data: { label: "Parent", condition: "x" },
    };
    expect(sortParentsFirst([child, parent])).toEqual([parent, child]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 12: Implement flow-convert.ts**

```ts
// lib/flow-convert.ts
import type { Edge, Node } from "@xyflow/react";
import { parseHandleId } from "@/components/flow-kit/flow-types";
import type { AnyKindData, Flow, FlowEdge, FlowMeta, FlowNode, FlowViewport, NodeKind } from "./flow-schema";

export type BuilderNode = Node<AnyKindData, NodeKind>;
export type BuilderEdge = Edge<{ port?: string; streaming?: boolean }, "typed">;

export function toBuilderNode(n: FlowNode): BuilderNode {
  const node: BuilderNode = {
    id: n.id,
    type: n.kind,
    position: n.position,
    data: n.data,
  };
  if (n.parentId !== undefined) {
    node.parentId = n.parentId;
    node.extent = "parent";
  }
  if (n.kind === "note") node.zIndex = -1;
  if (n.width !== undefined) node.width = n.width;
  if (n.height !== undefined) node.height = n.height;
  return node;
}

export function fromBuilderNode(n: BuilderNode): FlowNode {
  const node: FlowNode = {
    id: n.id,
    kind: n.type as NodeKind,
    position: n.position,
    data: n.data,
  };
  if (n.parentId !== undefined) node.parentId = n.parentId;
  if (n.width !== undefined) node.width = n.width;
  if (n.height !== undefined) node.height = n.height;
  return node;
}

export function toBuilderEdge(e: FlowEdge): BuilderEdge {
  const port = parseHandleId(e.sourceHandle)?.port;
  return {
    id: e.id,
    type: "typed",
    source: e.source,
    target: e.target,
    sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,
    targetHandle: e.targetHandle,
    ...(e.label !== undefined && { label: e.label }),
    data: { ...(port !== undefined && { port }) },
  };
}

export function fromBuilderEdge(e: BuilderEdge): FlowEdge {
  const edge: FlowEdge = {
    id: e.id,
    source: e.source,
    target: e.target,
    sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle ?? "",
    targetHandle: e.targetHandle ?? "",
  };
  if (typeof e.label === "string") edge.label = e.label;
  return edge;
}

export function sortParentsFirst(nodes: BuilderNode[]): BuilderNode[] {
  const byId = new Map(nodes.map((n) => [n.id, n]));
  const depth = (n: BuilderNode): number => {
    let d = 0;
    let current: BuilderNode | undefined = n;
    while (current?.parentId) {
      current = byId.get(current.parentId);
      d++;
    }
    return d;
  };
  return nodes
    .map((n, i) => ({ n, i, d: depth(n) }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.d - b.d || a.i - b.i)
    .map((x) => x.n);
}

export function flowToGraph(flow: Flow): { nodes: BuilderNode[]; edges: BuilderEdge[] } {
  return {
    nodes: sortParentsFirst(flow.nodes.map(toBuilderNode)),
    edges: flow.edges.map(toBuilderEdge),
  };
}

export function graphToFlow(
  meta: FlowMeta,
  nodes: BuilderNode[],
  edges: BuilderEdge[],
  viewport?: FlowViewport,
): Flow {
  return {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    ...meta,
    nodes: sortParentsFirst(nodes).map(fromBuilderNode),
    edges: edges.map(fromBuilderEdge),
    ...(viewport !== undefined && { viewport }),
  };
}

export function canonicalFlowJson(flow: Flow): string {
  return JSON.stringify(sortKeysDeep(flow), null, 2) + "\n";
}

function sortKeysDeep(value: unknown): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(sortKeysDeep);
  if (value !== null && typeof value === "object") {
    const sorted: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const key of Object.keys(value as Record<string, unknown>).sort()) {
      sorted[key] = sortKeysDeep((value as Record<string, unknown>)[key]);
    }
    return sorted;
  }
  return value;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 16: Implement flows.server.ts**

```ts
// lib/flows.server.ts
import { readdir, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { parseFlow, type Flow } from "./flow-schema";

export const FLOWS_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), "flows");

export async function listFlows(dir: string = FLOWS_DIR): Promise<Flow[]> {
  const entries = await readdir(dir);
  const files = entries.filter((f) => f.endsWith(".json")).sort();
  const flows: Flow[] = [];
  for (const file of files) {
    const raw = await readFile(path.join(dir, file), "utf8");
    const result = parseFlow(JSON.parse(raw));
    if (!result.ok) {
      throw new Error(`${file}: ${result.errors.join("; ")}`);
    }
    flows.push(result.flow);
  }
  return flows;
}

export async function getFlow(slug: string, dir: string = FLOWS_DIR): Promise<Flow | null> {
  const file = path.join(dir, `${slug}.json`);
  let raw: string;
  try {
    raw = await readFile(file, "utf8");
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  const result = parseFlow(JSON.parse(raw));
  if (!result.ok) {
    throw new Error(`${slug}.json: ${result.errors.join("; ")}`);
  }
  return result.flow;
}
```

  ```ts
    | { type: "text"; key: string; label: string; placeholder?: string }
  ```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the node catalog**

Create `components/builder/node-catalog.ts`:

```ts
import {
  Play,
  Bot,
  ListChecks,
  Square,
  StickyNote,
  FileSearch,
  ShieldCheck,
  Plug,
  GitBranch,
  Repeat,
  UserCheck,
  Shuffle,
  Database,
  type LucideIcon,
} from "lucide-react";
import type { HandleDir } from "@/components/flow-kit/flow-types";
import { NODE_KINDS, type NodeKind, type NodeGroup, type KindData } from "@/lib/flow-schema";
import type { BuilderNode } from "@/lib/flow-convert";

export type PortSpec = { dir: HandleDir; port?: string; label?: string };

export type FieldDescriptor =
  | { type: "text"; key: string; label: string; placeholder?: string }
  | { type: "textarea"; key: string; label: string; placeholder?: string }
  | { type: "number"; key: string; label: string; min?: number }
  | { type: "select"; key: string; label: string; options: { value: string; label: string }[] }
  | { type: "chips"; key: string; label: string }
  | { type: "string-list"; key: string; label: string; itemLabel: string; min?: number }
  | { type: "condition-list"; key: "conditions"; label: string }
  | { type: "kv-list"; key: "assignments"; label: string };

export type Renderer = "kind" | "terminal" | "branch" | "while" | "note";

export interface CatalogEntry<K extends NodeKind = NodeKind> {
  kind: K;
  label: string;
  group: NodeGroup;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  renderer: Renderer;
  subtitle?: (data: KindData[K]) => string | undefined;
  defaultData: () => KindData[K];
  ports: (data: KindData[K]) => PortSpec[];
  fields: FieldDescriptor[];
  defaultSize?: { width: number; height: number };
}

// Every kind gets the same leading label field and trailing description field; kind-specific
// fields are sandwiched between the two. Implemented once here rather than repeated 13 times.
function withCommonFields(fields: FieldDescriptor[]): FieldDescriptor[] {
  return [
    { type: "text", key: "label", label: "Label" },
    ...fields,
    { type: "textarea", key: "description", label: "Description" },
  ];
}

// Inspector shows a human label for the trigger enum; also doubles as the start node's subtitle.
const TRIGGER_LABELS: Record<KindData["start"]["trigger"], string> = {
  manual: "Manual",
  schedule: "Schedule",
  webhook: "Webhook",
  event: "Event",
};

function truncate(s: string, max: number): string {
  return s.length > max ? `${s.slice(0, max - 3)}...` : s;
}

export const NODE_CATALOG: { [K in NodeKind]: CatalogEntry<K> } = {
  start: {
    kind: "start",
    label: "Start",
    group: "core",
    icon: Play,
    renderer: "terminal",
    subtitle: (data) => TRIGGER_LABELS[data.trigger],
    defaultData: () => ({ label: "Start", trigger: "manual" }),
    ports: () => [{ dir: "out" }],
    fields: withCommonFields([
      {
        type: "select",
        key: "trigger",
        label: "Trigger",
        options: [
          { value: "manual", label: "Manual" },
          { value: "schedule", label: "Schedule" },
          { value: "webhook", label: "Webhook" },
          { value: "event", label: "Event" },
        ],
      },
      { type: "text", key: "triggerDetail", label: "Trigger detail" },
    ]),
  },

  agent: {
    kind: "agent",
    label: "Agent",
    group: "core",
    icon: Bot,
    renderer: "kind",
    subtitle: (data) => data.model ?? "Agent",
    defaultData: () => ({ label: "Agent", instructions: "" }),
    ports: () => [{ dir: "in" }, { dir: "out" }],
    fields: withCommonFields([
      { type: "textarea", key: "instructions", label: "Instructions" },
      { type: "text", key: "model", label: "Model" },
      { type: "chips", key: "tools", label: "Tools" },
    ]),
  },

  classify: {
    kind: "classify",
    label: "Classify",
    group: "core",
    icon: ListChecks,

  },
};

export const PALETTE_GROUPS: { group: NodeGroup; label: string; kinds: NodeKind[] }[] = [
  { group: "core", label: "Core", kinds: ["start", "agent", "classify", "end", "note"] },
  { group: "tools", label: "Tools", kinds: ["file-search", "guardrails", "mcp"] },
  { group: "logic", label: "Logic", kinds: ["if-else", "while", "user-approval"] },
  { group: "data", label: "Data", kinds: ["transform", "set-state"] },
];

// Sanity check that PALETTE_GROUPS and NODE_KINDS never drift apart silently.
if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production") {
  const flattened = PALETTE_GROUPS.flatMap((g) => g.kinds);
  if (flattened.length !== NODE_KINDS.length || !NODE_KINDS.every((k) => flattened.includes(k))) {
    console.warn("PALETTE_GROUPS is missing or duplicating a kind from NODE_KINDS");
  }
}

export function catalogFor(kind: NodeKind): CatalogEntry {
  // NODE_CATALOG[kind] is a union of CatalogEntry<K> across every K; narrowing it back to the
  // widened CatalogEntry (K defaulted to NodeKind) is the same widening portsFor/portLabel below
  // rely on, since kind is a runtime string here, not a type-level literal.
  return NODE_CATALOG[kind] as CatalogEntry;
}

export function portsFor(node: BuilderNode): PortSpec[] {
  return catalogFor(node.type).ports(node.data);
}

export function portLabel(node: BuilderNode, port: string | undefined): string | undefined {
  return portsFor(node).find((p) => p.port === port)?.label;
}
```

### Task 5: Store, storage, draft persistence, connection rules

Split into four sub-tasks (5a-5d) because each produces an independently reviewable and
committable file: a reviewer could accept the connection-rule matrix while still wanting
changes to the undo-coalescing logic, or accept the store while asking for changes to the
debounce timing. Build them in order. 5c depends on 5a and on Task 4's `catalogFor`; 5d

```ts
import { isValidBuilderConnection, edgeIdFor } from "./connection-rules";

function node(id: string, parentId?: string): BuilderNode {

      source: "c1",
      target: "d1",
      sourceHandle: portHandleId("c1", "out"),
      targetHandle: portHandleId("d1", "in"),
    };
    expect(isValidBuilderConnection(c, nodes)).toBe(false);
  });

  test("in to in rejected", () => {
    const c = {
      source: "a",
      target: "b",
      sourceHandle: portHandleId("a", "in"),
      targetHandle: portHandleId("b", "in"),

    expect(isValidBuilderConnection(c, nodes)).toBe(false);

      source: "a",
      target: "ghost",
      sourceHandle: portHandleId("a", "out"),
      targetHandle: portHandleId("ghost", "in"),

});

describe("edgeIdFor", () => {

      source: "a",
      target: "b",
      sourceHandle: portHandleId("a", "out"),
      targetHandle: portHandleId("b", "in"),
    };
    expect(edgeIdFor(c)).toBe("a->b");
  });

  test("with a port", () => {
    const c = {
      source: "a",
      target: "b",
      sourceHandle: portHandleId("a", "out", "c0"),
      targetHandle: portHandleId("b", "in"),
    };
    expect(edgeIdFor(c)).toBe("a@c0->b");
```
