- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the core top bar**

```tsx
// components/builder/top-bar.tsx
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import type { Flow } from "@/lib/flow-schema";
import { saveStatus, useFlowStore, useFlowStoreApi, type SaveStatus } from "@/lib/flow-store";

const STATUS_LABEL: Record<SaveStatus, string> = {
  saved: "Saved",
  draft: "Draft",
  "unsaved-draft": "Unsaved draft",
  "not-saving": "Not saving",
};

export function TopBar({ slug, file }: { slug: string; file: Flow | null }) {
  const name = useFlowStore((s) => s.meta.name);
  const mode = useFlowStore((s) => s.mode);
  const status = useFlowStore(saveStatus);
  const storeApi = useFlowStoreApi();
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const [draftName, setDraftName] = React.useState(name);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setDraftName(name);
  }, [name]);

  const commitName = () => {
    const trimmed = draftName.trim();
    if (trimmed && trimmed !== name) {
      storeApi.getState().setMeta({ name: trimmed });
    } else {
      setDraftName(name);
    }
  };

  const setModeAndUrl = (next: "edit" | "view") => {
    storeApi.getState().setMode(next);
    const params = new URLSearchParams(searchParams.toString());
    if (next === "view") {
      params.set("mode", "view");
    } else {
      params.delete("mode");
    }
    const query = params.toString();
    router.replace(query ? `${pathname}?${query}` : pathname);
  };

  const badge = (
    <Badge data-slot="status-badge" variant="secondary">
      {STATUS_LABEL[status]}
    </Badge>
  );

  return (
    <header data-slot="top-bar" className="flex h-12 items-center gap-3 border-b border-border bg-card px-3">
      <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" render={<Link href="/" aria-label="Back to flows" />}>
        <ArrowLeft className="size-4" />
      </Button>
      <Input
        value={draftName}
        onChange={(event) => setDraftName(event.target.value)}
        onBlur={commitName}
        onKeyDown={(event) => {
          if (event.key === "Enter") event.currentTarget.blur();
        }}
        aria-label="Flow name"
        className="h-7 w-48"
      />
      {status === "not-saving" ? (
        <Tooltip>
          <TooltipTrigger render={<span />}>{badge}</TooltipTrigger>
          <TooltipContent>Browser storage is unavailable; export to keep your work</TooltipContent>
        </Tooltip>
      ) : (
        badge
      )}
      <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1">
        <div className="flex rounded-lg border border-border p-0.5">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant={mode === "edit" ? "secondary" : "ghost"}
            size="sm"
            aria-pressed={mode === "edit"}
            onClick={() => setModeAndUrl("edit")}
          >
            Edit
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant={mode === "view" ? "secondary" : "ghost"}
            size="sm"
            aria-pressed={mode === "view"}
            onClick={() => setModeAndUrl("view")}
          >
            View
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

```tsx
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "View" }));

    expect(mockReplace).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/flow/my-flow?mode=view");
  });

  it("exports a file named <slug>.json", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const flow = fixtureFlow();
    const store = createFlowStore(flow, { file: flow });
    const createObjectURL = vi.fn(() => "blob:mock");
    const revokeObjectURL = vi.fn();
    URL.createObjectURL = createObjectURL;
    URL.revokeObjectURL = revokeObjectURL;
    const realCreateElement = document.createElement.bind(document);
    const downloads: string[] = [];
    vi.spyOn(document, "createElement").mockImplementation((tag: string) => {
      const el = realCreateElement(tag);
      if (tag === "a") {
        vi.spyOn(el as HTMLAnchorElement, "click").mockImplementation(() => {
          downloads.push((el as HTMLAnchorElement).download);
        });
      }
      return el;
    });
    renderWithStore(<TopBar slug="my-flow" file={flow} />, store);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "More options" }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Export JSON" }));
    expect(createObjectURL).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(downloads).toEqual(["my-flow.json"]);
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  it("shows the error list for invalid JSON and leaves the node count unchanged", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const flow = fixtureFlow();
    const store = createFlowStore(flow, { file: flow });
    const before = store.getState().nodes.length;
    renderWithStore(<TopBar slug="my-flow" file={flow} />, store);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "More options" }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Import JSON" }));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Flow JSON text"), "{ not json");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Import" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Invalid JSON/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(store.getState().nodes.length).toBe(before);
  });

  it("replaces nodes when importing a valid flow", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const flow = fixtureFlow();
    const store = createFlowStore(flow, { file: flow });
    const replacement: Flow = {
      schemaVersion: 1,
      id: "replacement",
      name: "Replacement",
      source: "manual",
      createdAt: "2026-01-02T00:00:00.000Z",
      updatedAt: "2026-01-02T00:00:00.000Z",
      nodes: [{ id: "start-only", kind: "start", position: { x: 0, y: 0 }, data: { label: "Start", trigger: "manual" } }],
      edges: [],
    };
    renderWithStore(<TopBar slug="my-flow" file={flow} />, store);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "More options" }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Import JSON" }));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText("Flow JSON text"), canonicalFlowJson(replacement));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Import" }));
    expect(store.getState().nodes).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(store.getState().nodes[0].id).toBe("start-only");
  });

  it("copies the view URL to the clipboard", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const flow = fixtureFlow();
    const store = createFlowStore(flow, { file: flow });
    const writeText = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    Object.assign(navigator, { clipboard: { writeText } });
    renderWithStore(<TopBar slug="my-flow" file={flow} />, store);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "More options" }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Copy share link" }));
    expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.stringContaining("/flow/my-flow?mode=view"));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the overflow menu**

```tsx
// components/builder/top-bar.tsx
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { ArrowLeft, MoreVertical } from "lucide-react";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { canonicalFlowJson } from "@/lib/flow-convert";
import { parseFlow, type Flow } from "@/lib/flow-schema";
import { deleteDraft } from "@/lib/storage";
import { saveStatus, useFlowStore, useFlowStoreApi, type SaveStatus } from "@/lib/flow-store";

const STATUS_LABEL: Record<SaveStatus, string> = {
  saved: "Saved",
  draft: "Draft",
  "unsaved-draft": "Unsaved draft",
  "not-saving": "Not saving",
};

function ImportDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  onImport,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  onImport: (flow: Flow) => void;
}) {
  const [text, setText] = React.useState("");
  const [errors, setErrors] = React.useState<string[] | null>(null);

  const handleFile = async (fileList: FileList | null) => {
    const file = fileList?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setText(await file.text());
  };

  const submit = () => {
    let parsed: unknown;
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(text);
    } catch {
      setErrors(["Invalid JSON: could not parse the input."]);
      return;
    }
    const result = parseFlow(parsed);
    if (!result.ok) {
      setErrors(result.errors);
      return;
    }
    setErrors(null);
    onImport(result.flow);
    onOpenChange(false);
    setText("");
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent>
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Import flow JSON</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>Choose a file or paste flow JSON below.</DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <input
          type="file"
          accept="application/json"
          aria-label="Flow JSON file"
          onChange={(event) => void handleFile(event.target.files)}
        />
        <Textarea
          value={text}
          onChange={(event) => setText(event.target.value)}
          rows={10}
          aria-label="Flow JSON text"
          className="font-mono text-xs"
        />
        {errors && (
          <ul
            data-slot="import-errors"
            className="list-disc space-y-1 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 p-2 pl-6 text-xs text-destructive"
          >
            {errors.map((error) => (
              <li key={error}>{error}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
        <DialogFooter>
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button type="button" onClick={submit} disabled={!text.trim()}>
            Import
          </Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

export function TopBar({ slug, file }: { slug: string; file: Flow | null }) {
  const name = useFlowStore((s) => s.meta.name);
  const mode = useFlowStore((s) => s.mode);
  const status = useFlowStore(saveStatus);
  const storeApi = useFlowStoreApi();
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const [draftName, setDraftName] = React.useState(name);
  const [importOpen, setImportOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [copied, setCopied] = React.useState(false);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setDraftName(name);
  }, [name]);

  const commitName = () => {
    const trimmed = draftName.trim();
    if (trimmed && trimmed !== name) {
      storeApi.getState().setMeta({ name: trimmed });
    } else {
      setDraftName(name);
    }
  };

  const setModeAndUrl = (next: "edit" | "view") => {
    storeApi.getState().setMode(next);
    const params = new URLSearchParams(searchParams.toString());
    if (next === "view") {
      params.set("mode", "view");
    } else {
      params.delete("mode");
    }
    const query = params.toString();
    router.replace(query ? `${pathname}?${query}` : pathname);
  };

  const exportJson = () => {
    const json = canonicalFlowJson(storeApi.getState().exportFlow());
    const blob = new Blob([json], { type: "application/json" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const anchor = document.createElement("a");
    anchor.href = url;
    anchor.download = `${slug}.json`;
    anchor.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const copyShareLink = async () => {
    const url = `${window.location.origin}/flow/${slug}?mode=view`;
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
    setCopied(true);
    window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500);
  };

  const revertToFile = () => {
    if (!file) return;
    storeApi.getState().importFlow(file);
    deleteDraft(slug);
  };

  const deleteDraftAndReset = () => {
    deleteDraft(slug);
    if (file) {
      storeApi.getState().importFlow(file);
    } else {
      router.push("/");
    }
  };

  const badge = (
    <Badge data-slot="status-badge" variant="secondary">
      {STATUS_LABEL[status]}
    </Badge>
  );

  return (
    <header data-slot="top-bar" className="flex h-12 items-center gap-3 border-b border-border bg-card px-3">
      <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" render={<Link href="/" aria-label="Back to flows" />}>
        <ArrowLeft className="size-4" />
      </Button>
      <Input
        value={draftName}
        onChange={(event) => setDraftName(event.target.value)}
        onBlur={commitName}
        onKeyDown={(event) => {
          if (event.key === "Enter") event.currentTarget.blur();
        }}
        aria-label="Flow name"
        className="h-7 w-48"
      />
      {status === "not-saving" ? (
        <Tooltip>
          <TooltipTrigger render={<span />}>{badge}</TooltipTrigger>
          <TooltipContent>Browser storage is unavailable; export to keep your work</TooltipContent>
        </Tooltip>
      ) : (
        badge
      )}
      <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1">
        <div className="flex rounded-lg border border-border p-0.5">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant={mode === "edit" ? "secondary" : "ghost"}
            size="sm"
            aria-pressed={mode === "edit"}
            onClick={() => setModeAndUrl("edit")}
          >
            Edit
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant={mode === "view" ? "secondary" : "ghost"}
            size="sm"
            aria-pressed={mode === "view"}
            onClick={() => setModeAndUrl("view")}
          >
            View
          </Button>
        </div>
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger render={<Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" aria-label="More options" />}>
            <MoreVertical className="size-4" />
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent align="end">
            <DropdownMenuItem onClick={exportJson}>Export JSON</DropdownMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => setImportOpen(true)}>Import JSON</DropdownMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => void copyShareLink()}>
              {copied ? "Copied" : "Copy share link"}
            </DropdownMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenuSeparator />
            <DropdownMenuItem disabled={!file} onClick={revertToFile}>
              Revert to file
            </DropdownMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenuItem variant="destructive" onClick={deleteDraftAndReset}>
              Delete draft
            </DropdownMenuItem>
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>
      </div>
      <ImportDialog
        open={importOpen}
        onOpenChange={setImportOpen}
        onImport={(flow) => storeApi.getState().importFlow(flow)}
      />
    </header>
  );
}
```

  ```ts
  export type FieldDescriptor =
    | { type: "text"; key: string; label: string; placeholder?: string }
    | { type: "textarea"; key: string; label: string; placeholder?: string }
    | { type: "number"; key: string; label: string; min?: number }
  ```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the shell and the simple field editors**

```tsx
// components/builder/node-inspector.tsx
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { Copy, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { catalogFor, type FieldDescriptor } from "@/components/builder/node-catalog";
import type { AnyKindData } from "@/lib/flow-schema";
import type { BuilderNode } from "@/lib/flow-convert";
import { useFlowStore, useFlowStoreApi } from "@/lib/flow-store";

function LabeledField({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
      {label}
      {children}
    </label>
  );
}

function fieldValue(data: AnyKindData, key: string): unknown {
  return (data as Record<string, unknown>)[key];
}

function FieldEditor({
  field,
  data,
  onChange,
}: {
  field: FieldDescriptor;
  data: AnyKindData;
  onChange: (patch: Partial<AnyKindData>) => void;
}) {
  switch (field.type) {
    case "text":
      return (
        <LabeledField label={field.label}>
          <Input
            value={String(fieldValue(data, field.key) ?? "")}
            placeholder={field.placeholder}
            onChange={(event) => onChange({ [field.key]: event.target.value } as Partial<AnyKindData>)}
          />
        </LabeledField>
      );
    case "textarea":
      return (
        <LabeledField label={field.label}>
          <Textarea
            value={String(fieldValue(data, field.key) ?? "")}
            placeholder={field.placeholder}
            rows={4}
            onChange={(event) => onChange({ [field.key]: event.target.value } as Partial<AnyKindData>)}
          />
        </LabeledField>
      );
    case "number":
      return (
        <LabeledField label={field.label}>
          <Input
            type="number"
            min={field.min}
            value={String(fieldValue(data, field.key) ?? "")}
            onChange={(event) =>
              onChange({
                [field.key]: event.target.value === "" ? undefined : Number(event.target.value),
              } as Partial<AnyKindData>)
            }
          />
        </LabeledField>
      );
    case "select":
      return (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
          <span>{field.label}</span>
          <Select
            value={String(fieldValue(data, field.key) ?? "")}
            onValueChange={(value) => onChange({ [field.key]: value } as Partial<AnyKindData>)}
          >
            <SelectTrigger aria-label={field.label}>
              <SelectValue />
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              {field.options.map((option) => (
                <SelectItem key={option.value} value={option.value}>
                  {option.label}
                </SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
        </div>
      );
    default:
      return null; // chips, string-list, condition-list, kv-list: Task 10b
  }
}

function SingleSelectPanel({ node }: { node: BuilderNode }) {
  const storeApi = useFlowStoreApi();
  const entry = catalogFor(node.type);
  const Icon = entry.icon;

  const onFieldChange = (patch: Partial<AnyKindData>) => {
    storeApi.getState().updateNodeData(node.id, patch);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <div
          className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-white"
          style={{ background: `var(--flow-${entry.group})` }}
        >
          <Icon className="size-4" />
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="truncate text-sm font-medium">{node.data.label}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{entry.label}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <LabeledField label="Label">
        <Input
          value={node.data.label}
          onChange={(event) => onFieldChange({ label: event.target.value } as Partial<AnyKindData>)}
        />
      </LabeledField>
      {entry.fields.map((field) => (
        <FieldEditor key={field.key} field={field} data={node.data} onChange={onFieldChange} />
      ))}
      <div className="flex gap-2 border-t border-border pt-3">
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="outline"
          size="sm"
          className="flex-1"
          onClick={() => storeApi.getState().duplicateSelection()}
        >
          <Copy className="size-3.5" /> Duplicate
        </Button>
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="destructive"
          size="sm"
          className="flex-1"
          onClick={() => storeApi.getState().removeSelection()}
        >
          <Trash2 className="size-3.5" /> Delete
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function MultiSelectPanel({ count, onDelete }: { count: number; onDelete: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <div className="text-sm font-medium">{count} nodes selected</div>
      <Button type="button" variant="destructive" size="sm" onClick={onDelete}>
        Delete
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

export function NodeInspector({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const selection = useFlowStore((s) => s.nodes.filter((n) => n.selected));
  const storeApi = useFlowStoreApi();

  if (selection.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div
      data-slot="inspector"
      className={cn(
        "absolute top-4 right-4 max-h-[calc(100%-2rem)] w-80 overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-3 shadow-md",
        className,
      )}
    >
      {selection.length > 1 ? (
        <MultiSelectPanel count={selection.length} onDelete={() => storeApi.getState().removeSelection()} />
      ) : (
        <SingleSelectPanel node={selection[0]} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// components/builder/node-inspector.test.tsx
// (append to the file written in Task 10a; the full file now reads:)
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { act, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { createFlowStore } from "@/lib/flow-store";
import { fixtureFlow, renderWithStore } from "@/lib/test-fixtures";
import { portsFor } from "@/components/builder/node-catalog";
import type { AgentData } from "@/lib/flow-schema";
import type { Flow } from "@/lib/flow-schema";
import { NodeInspector } from "./node-inspector";

function selectOnly(store: ReturnType<typeof createFlowStore>, id: string) {
  act(() => {
    store.setState((s) => ({ nodes: s.nodes.map((n) => ({ ...n, selected: n.id === id })) }));
  });
}

const ifElseFlow: Flow = {
  schemaVersion: 1,
  id: "if-else-fixture",
  name: "If-else fixture",
  source: "manual",
  createdAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
  updatedAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
  nodes: [
    {
      id: "if-1",
      kind: "if-else",
      position: { x: 0, y: 0 },
      data: { label: "Check", conditions: [{ label: "Ready", expression: "state.ready == true" }] },
    },
  ],
  edges: [],
};

const classifyFlow: Flow = {
  schemaVersion: 1,
  id: "classify-fixture",
  name: "Classify fixture",
  source: "manual",
  createdAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
  updatedAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
  nodes: [{ id: "classify-1", kind: "classify", position: { x: 0, y: 0 }, data: { label: "Route", categories: ["personal"] } }],
  edges: [],
};

describe("NodeInspector", () => {
  it("renders nothing when no node is selected", () => {
    const store = createFlowStore(fixtureFlow());
    const { container } = renderWithStore(<NodeInspector />, store);
    expect(container.querySelector('[data-slot="inspector"]')).toBeNull();
  });

  it("shows the count and deletes all selected nodes on multi-select Delete", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const store = createFlowStore(fixtureFlow());
    act(() => {
      store.setState((s) => ({ nodes: s.nodes.map((n) => ({ ...n, selected: true })) }));
    });
    const count = store.getState().nodes.length;
    renderWithStore(<NodeInspector />, store);
    expect(screen.getByText(`${count} nodes selected`)).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete" }));
    expect(store.getState().nodes).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("updates the store when the label input changes", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const store = createFlowStore(fixtureFlow());
    const target = store.getState().nodes.find((n) => n.type === "agent")!;
    selectOnly(store, target.id);
    renderWithStore(<NodeInspector />, store);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText("Label");
    await user.clear(input);
    await user.type(input, "Renamed agent");
    expect(store.getState().nodes.find((n) => n.id === target.id)?.data.label).toBe("Renamed agent");
  });

  it("adds a condition on an if-else node, increasing its out ports by one", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const store = createFlowStore(ifElseFlow);
    const target = store.getState().nodes[0];
    const before = portsFor(target).filter((p) => p.dir === "out").length;
    selectOnly(store, target.id);
    renderWithStore(<NodeInspector />, store);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Add condition" }));
    const updated = store.getState().nodes.find((n) => n.id === target.id)!;
    const after = portsFor(updated).filter((p) => p.dir === "out").length;
    expect(after).toBe(before + 1);
  });

  it("disables removing the last classify category", () => {
    const store = createFlowStore(classifyFlow);
    selectOnly(store, store.getState().nodes[0].id);
    renderWithStore(<NodeInspector />, store);
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove Category 1" })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it("adds a tool chip on Enter", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const store = createFlowStore(fixtureFlow());
    const target = store.getState().nodes.find((n) => n.type === "agent")!;
    selectOnly(store, target.id);
    renderWithStore(<NodeInspector />, store);
    const input = screen.getByPlaceholderText("Add and press Enter");
    await user.type(input, "bash{Enter}");
    const updated = store.getState().nodes.find((n) => n.id === target.id)!;
    expect((updated.data as AgentData).tools).toContain("bash");
  });

  it("duplicates the selected node", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const store = createFlowStore(fixtureFlow());
    const target = store.getState().nodes.find((n) => n.type === "agent")!;
    selectOnly(store, target.id);
    renderWithStore(<NodeInspector />, store);
    const before = store.getState().nodes.length;
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Duplicate" }));
    expect(store.getState().nodes.length).toBe(before + 1);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the list field editors**

```tsx
// components/builder/node-inspector.tsx
// Add these imports alongside the Task 10a imports:
import type { AgentData, ClassifyData, GuardrailsData, IfElseData, SetStateData } from "@/lib/flow-schema";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";

// Add these components above FieldEditor, then extend FieldEditor's switch:

function ChipsField({
  field,
  data,
  onChange,
}: {
  field: Extract<FieldDescriptor, { type: "chips" }>;
  data: AnyKindData;
  onChange: (patch: Partial<AnyKindData>) => void;
}) {
  const values = (fieldValue(data, field.key) as string[] | undefined) ?? [];
  const [draft, setDraft] = React.useState("");

  const commit = () => {
    const value = draft.trim();
    if (!value) return;
    onChange({ [field.key]: [...values, value] } as Partial<AnyKindData>);
    setDraft("");
  };

  const remove = (index: number) => {
    onChange({ [field.key]: values.filter((_, i) => i !== index) } as Partial<AnyKindData>);
  };

  return (
    <LabeledField label={field.label}>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
        {values.map((value, index) => (
          <Badge key={`${value}-${index}`} variant="secondary" className="gap-1">
            {value}
            <button type="button" aria-label={`Remove ${value}`} onClick={() => remove(index)} className="ml-0.5">
              &times;
            </button>
          </Badge>
        ))}
      </div>
      <Input
        value={draft}
        placeholder="Add and press Enter"
        onChange={(event) => setDraft(event.target.value)}
        onKeyDown={(event) => {
          if (event.key === "Enter") {
            event.preventDefault();
            commit();
          }
        }}
      />
    </LabeledField>
  );
}

function StringListField({
  field,
  data,
  onChange,
}: {
  field: Extract<FieldDescriptor, { type: "string-list" }>;
  data: AnyKindData;
  onChange: (patch: Partial<AnyKindData>) => void;
}) {
  const values = (fieldValue(data, field.key) as string[] | undefined) ?? [];
  const min = field.min ?? 0;

  const update = (index: number, value: string) => {
    onChange({ [field.key]: values.map((v, i) => (i === index ? value : v)) } as Partial<AnyKindData>);
  };
  const add = () => {
    onChange({ [field.key]: [...values, ""] } as Partial<AnyKindData>);
  };
  const remove = (index: number) => {
    onChange({ [field.key]: values.filter((_, i) => i !== index) } as Partial<AnyKindData>);
  };

  return (
    <LabeledField label={field.label}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {values.map((value, index) => (
          <div key={index} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Input aria-label={`${field.itemLabel} ${index + 1}`} value={value} onChange={(event) => update(index, event.target.value)} />
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="ghost"
              size="icon-sm"
              aria-label={`Remove ${field.itemLabel} ${index + 1}`}
              disabled={values.length <= min}
              onClick={() => remove(index)}
            >
              <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
            </Button>
          </div>
        ))}
        <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={add}>
          Add {field.itemLabel}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </LabeledField>
  );
}

function ConditionListField({
  data,
  onChange,
}: {
  data: AnyKindData;
  onChange: (patch: Partial<AnyKindData>) => void;
}) {
  const conditions = (data as IfElseData).conditions;
  const min = 1;

  const update = (index: number, patch: Partial<{ label: string; expression: string }>) => {
    onChange({ conditions: conditions.map((c, i) => (i === index ? { ...c, ...patch } : c)) });
  };
  const add = () => {
    onChange({ conditions: [...conditions, { label: `Condition ${conditions.length + 1}`, expression: "" }] });
  };
  const remove = (index: number) => {
    onChange({ conditions: conditions.filter((_, i) => i !== index) });
  };

  return (
    <LabeledField label="Conditions">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        {conditions.map((condition, index) => (
          <div key={index} className="flex flex-col gap-1 rounded-lg border border-border p-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Input aria-label={`Condition ${index + 1} label`} value={condition.label} onChange={(event) => update(index, { label: event.target.value })} />
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="ghost"
                size="icon-sm"
                aria-label={`Remove condition ${index + 1}`}
                disabled={conditions.length <= min}
                onClick={() => remove(index)}
              >
                <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
              </Button>
            </div>
            <Input
              aria-label={`Condition ${index + 1} expression`}
              value={condition.expression}
              placeholder="state.ready == true"
              className="font-mono text-xs"
              onChange={(event) => update(index, { expression: event.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
        ))}
        <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={add}>
          Add condition
        </Button>
      </div>
    </LabeledField>
  );
}

function KvListField({ data, onChange }: { data: AnyKindData; onChange: (patch: Partial<AnyKindData>) => void }) {
  const assignments = (data as SetStateData).assignments;

  const update = (index: number, patch: Partial<{ key: string; value: string }>) => {
    onChange({ assignments: assignments.map((a, i) => (i === index ? { ...a, ...patch } : a)) });
  };
  const add = () => {
    onChange({ assignments: [...assignments, { key: "", value: "" }] });
  };
  const remove = (index: number) => {
    onChange({ assignments: assignments.filter((_, i) => i !== index) });
  };

  return (
    <LabeledField label="Assignments">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {assignments.map((assignment, index) => (
          <div key={index} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Input aria-label={`Assignment ${index + 1} key`} value={assignment.key} placeholder="key" onChange={(event) => update(index, { key: event.target.value })} />
            <Input aria-label={`Assignment ${index + 1} value`} value={assignment.value} placeholder="value" onChange={(event) => update(index, { value: event.target.value })} />
            <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" aria-label={`Remove assignment ${index + 1}`} onClick={() => remove(index)}>
              <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
            </Button>
          </div>
        ))}
        <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={add}>
          Add assignment
        </Button>
      </div>
    </LabeledField>
  );
}

//   case "kv-list": return <KvListField data={data} onChange={onChange} />;
```

#### Task 11a: Builder keyboard shortcuts

  ```ts
  export function useFlowStore<T>(selector: (s: FlowState) => T): T;
  export function useFlowStoreApi(): FlowStore;
  export function useFlowTemporal(): { undo: () => void; redo: () => void; canUndo: boolean; canRedo: boolean };
  ```

- Produces:

  ```ts
  // components/builder/use-builder-shortcuts.ts
  export function useBuilderShortcuts(): void;   // Delete/Backspace removeSelection · Mod+Z undo · Shift+Mod+Z redo · Mod+D duplicate · Escape deselect · V select tool · H hand tool; ignored while focus is in input, textarea, select, or contenteditable; no-op in view mode
  ```
  Mounted by `flow-builder.tsx` in Task 11b.

Escape deselect is implemented as an `applyNodeChanges` batch of `{ type: "select", selected: false }` entries, one per currently selected node, rather than a dedicated store action, matching the skeleton's note that Escape "is implemented as an applyNodeChanges select:false batch."

```ts
// components/builder/use-builder-shortcuts.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { renderHook } from "@testing-library/react";
import * as React from "react";
import { createFlowStore, FlowStoreProvider, type FlowStore } from "@/lib/flow-store";
import { fixtureFlow } from "@/lib/test-fixtures";
import { useBuilderShortcuts } from "./use-builder-shortcuts";

function wrapperFor(store: FlowStore) {
  return function Wrapper({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    return React.createElement(FlowStoreProvider, { store }, children);
  };
}

function dispatchKey(key: string, opts: KeyboardEventInit = {}, target: EventTarget = window) {
  target.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key, bubbles: true, cancelable: true, ...opts }));
}

describe("useBuilderShortcuts", () => {
  it("Mod+Z undoes the last store action", () => {
    const store = createFlowStore(fixtureFlow());
    const id = store.getState().addNode("agent", { x: 0, y: 0 });
    renderHook(() => useBuilderShortcuts(), { wrapper: wrapperFor(store) });

    dispatchKey("z", { metaKey: true });

    expect(store.getState().nodes.some((n) => n.id === id)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("Delete removes the selected node", () => {
    const store = createFlowStore(fixtureFlow());
    const target = store.getState().nodes[0];
    store.setState((s) => ({ nodes: s.nodes.map((n) => ({ ...n, selected: n.id === target.id })) }));
    renderHook(() => useBuilderShortcuts(), { wrapper: wrapperFor(store) });

    dispatchKey("Delete");

    expect(store.getState().nodes.some((n) => n.id === target.id)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("ignores a keystroke dispatched from an input element", () => {
    const store = createFlowStore(fixtureFlow());
    const before = store.getState().nodes.length;
    const target = store.getState().nodes[0];
    store.setState((s) => ({ nodes: s.nodes.map((n) => ({ ...n, selected: n.id === target.id })) }));
    renderHook(() => useBuilderShortcuts(), { wrapper: wrapperFor(store) });
    const input = document.createElement("input");
    document.body.appendChild(input);

    dispatchKey("Delete", {}, input);
    input.remove();

    expect(store.getState().nodes.length).toBe(before);
  });

  it("does nothing when mode is view", () => {

    const target = store.getState().nodes[0];

    dispatchKey("Delete");

    expect(store.getState().nodes.length).toBe(before);

});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the hook**

```ts
// components/builder/use-builder-shortcuts.ts
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import type { NodeChange } from "@xyflow/react";
import { useFlowStore, useFlowStoreApi, useFlowTemporal } from "@/lib/flow-store";
import type { BuilderNode } from "@/lib/flow-convert";

const EDITABLE_TAGS = new Set(["INPUT", "TEXTAREA", "SELECT"]);

function isEditableTarget(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  if (!(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
  return EDITABLE_TAGS.has(target.tagName) || target.isContentEditable;
}

export function useBuilderShortcuts(): void {
  const mode = useFlowStore((s) => s.mode);
  const storeApi = useFlowStoreApi();
  const { undo, redo } = useFlowTemporal();

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (mode !== "edit") return;

    function onKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (isEditableTarget(event.target)) return;
      const mod = event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey;
      const key = event.key.toLowerCase();

      if (event.key === "Delete" || event.key === "Backspace") {
        event.preventDefault();
        storeApi.getState().removeSelection();
        return;
      }
      if (mod && event.shiftKey && key === "z") {
        event.preventDefault();
        redo();
        return;
      }
      if (mod && key === "z") {
        event.preventDefault();
        undo();
        return;
      }
      if (mod && key === "d") {
        event.preventDefault();
        storeApi.getState().duplicateSelection();
        return;
      }
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        event.preventDefault();
        const { nodes, applyNodeChanges } = storeApi.getState();
        const changes: NodeChange<BuilderNode>[] = nodes
          .filter((n) => n.selected)
          .map((n) => ({ id: n.id, type: "select", selected: false }));
        if (changes.length > 0) applyNodeChanges(changes);
        return;
      }
      if (key === "v") {
        storeApi.getState().setTool("select");
        return;
      }
      if (key === "h") {
        storeApi.getState().setTool("hand");
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [mode, storeApi, undo, redo]);
}
```

#### Task 11b: Flow-builder client shell

**Files:**
- Create: `components/builder/flow-builder.tsx`
- Test: `components/builder/flow-builder.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  ```ts
  // lib/flow-schema.ts
  export type Flow = { /* ...as in the skeleton... */ };

  // lib/storage.ts
  export function loadDraft(slug: string): Flow | null;

  // lib/draft-persistence.ts
  export function bindDraftPersistence(store: FlowStore, slug: string, opts?: { debounceMs?: number }): () => void;

  // lib/flow-store.ts
  export type Mode = "edit" | "view";
  export type FlowStore = StoreApi<FlowState> & { temporal: StoreApi<TemporalState<Partial<FlowState>>> };
  export function createFlowStore(initial: Flow, opts?: { file?: Flow | null; mode?: Mode }): FlowStore;
  export function FlowStoreProvider(props: { store: FlowStore; children: React.ReactNode }): JSX.Element;
  export function useFlowStore<T>(selector: (s: FlowState) => T): T;
  ```
  plus `useBuilderShortcuts` (Task 11a) and, from Part C: `TopBar(props: { slug: string; file: Flow | null })`, `NodePalette(props: { className?: string })`, `NodeInspector(props: { className?: string })`, `CanvasToolbar(props: { className?: string })`, `FlowCanvas(props: { className?: string; children?: React.ReactNode })`.
- Produces:
  ```ts
  // components/builder/flow-builder.tsx
  export function FlowBuilder(props: { slug: string; file: Flow | null; initialMode: Mode }): JSX.Element;
  // resolves initial flow = loadDraft(slug) ?? file; if both null renders <FlowNotFound slug/> (a link back to "/"); creates the store once (useState initializer), wraps in FlowStoreProvider, binds persistence in an effect (edit mode only), mounts useBuilderShortcuts, renders TopBar + NodePalette + FlowCanvas + CanvasToolbar + NodeInspector in edit mode and only FlowCanvas in view mode.
  ```
  Rendered by `app/flow/[slug]/page.tsx` in Task 11d.

Two points the skeleton states as outcomes but does not spell out as mechanism, settled here:

1. **Hydration safety.** The server renders from `file` alone, since it has no access to the browser's `localStorage`. Reading `loadDraft` during the initial render would make the client's first paint disagree with whatever the server sent down, and React reports that mismatch as an error. So the draft lookup happens only inside a `useEffect` (which never runs on the server and never runs during the render that produces the first client paint), and the component renders exactly what the server rendered until that effect resolves.
2. **A store built from nothing.** `createFlowStore` requires a real `Flow`, not `null`. When `file` is `null` (an unknown slug) there is nothing to build a store from until the mount effect checks `localStorage`, so the "pending" state renders nothing at all rather than attempting a store with no content. Once the effect resolves, a `<BuilderShell>` keyed differently from the pending one forces a fresh `useState` initializer, so a draft found after mount is what the store ends up holding, even when `file` was `null` throughout.

```tsx
// components/builder/flow-builder.test.tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { saveDraft } from "@/lib/storage";
import { fixtureFlow } from "@/lib/test-fixtures";
import { FlowBuilder } from "./flow-builder";

describe("FlowBuilder", () => {
  it("renders FlowNotFound when there is no file and no draft", async () => {
    render(<FlowBuilder slug="ghost" file={null} initialMode="edit" />);
    expect(await screen.findByText("Flow not found")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Back to flows" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/");
  });

  it("renders the given file's nodes directly in edit mode when no draft exists", async () => {
    const flow = fixtureFlow();
    const { container } = render(<FlowBuilder slug={flow.id} file={flow} initialMode="edit" />);
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(container.querySelectorAll('[data-slot="node"]')).toHaveLength(flow.nodes.length);
    });
    expect(container.querySelector('[data-slot="node-palette"]')).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("renders a localStorage draft's nodes when file is null", async () => {
    const draft = fixtureFlow();
    saveDraft(draft);
    const { container } = render(<FlowBuilder slug={draft.id} file={null} initialMode="edit" />);
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(container.querySelectorAll('[data-slot="node"]')).toHaveLength(draft.nodes.length);
    });
  });

  it("renders only the canvas and an Edit link in view mode, no palette or inspector", async () => {
    const flow = fixtureFlow();
    const { container } = render(<FlowBuilder slug={flow.id} file={flow} initialMode="view" />);
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(container.querySelectorAll('[data-slot="node"]')).toHaveLength(flow.nodes.length);
    });
    expect(container.querySelector('[data-slot="node-palette"]')).toBeNull();
    expect(container.querySelector('[data-slot="inspector"]')).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "Edit" })).toHaveAttribute("href", `/flow/${flow.id}`);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the shell**

```tsx
// components/builder/flow-builder.tsx
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import type { Flow } from "@/lib/flow-schema";
import { loadDraft } from "@/lib/storage";
import { bindDraftPersistence } from "@/lib/draft-persistence";
import { createFlowStore, FlowStoreProvider, useFlowStore, type FlowStore, type Mode } from "@/lib/flow-store";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/builder/top-bar";
import { NodePalette } from "@/components/builder/node-palette";
import { NodeInspector } from "@/components/builder/node-inspector";
import { CanvasToolbar } from "@/components/builder/canvas-toolbar";
import { FlowCanvas } from "@/components/builder/flow-canvas";
import { useBuilderShortcuts } from "@/components/builder/use-builder-shortcuts";

function FlowNotFound({ slug }: { slug: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex h-dvh w-full items-center justify-center">
      <div
        data-slot="flow-not-found"
        className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-8 text-center shadow-sm"
      >
        <p className="text-sm font-medium">Flow not found</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          No committed or draft flow matches &quot;{slug}&quot;.
        </p>
        <Link href="/" className="text-sm underline underline-offset-4">
          Back to flows
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

type Resolution = { status: "pending" } | { status: "resolved"; flow: Flow } | { status: "missing" };

function BuilderChrome({ slug, file }: { slug: string; file: Flow | null }) {
  useBuilderShortcuts();
  const mode = useFlowStore((s) => s.mode);

  return (
    <div data-slot="flow-builder" className="relative flex h-dvh w-full flex-col">
      <TopBar slug={slug} file={file} />
      <FlowCanvas className="flex-1">
        {mode === "edit" ? (
          <>
            <NodePalette />
            <CanvasToolbar />
            <NodeInspector />
          </>
        ) : (
          <Link
            href={`/flow/${slug}`}
            data-slot="edit-link"
            className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-10 rounded-full border border-border bg-card px-3 py-1.5 text-sm shadow-md"
          >
            Edit
          </Link>
        )}
      </FlowCanvas>
    </div>
  );
}

function BuilderShell({
  slug,
  initial,
  file,
  initialMode,
}: {
  slug: string;
  initial: Flow;
  file: Flow | null;
  initialMode: Mode;
}) {
  const [store] = React.useState<FlowStore>(() => createFlowStore(initial, { file, mode: initialMode }));

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (initialMode !== "edit") return;
    return bindDraftPersistence(store, slug);
  }, [store, slug, initialMode]);

  return (
    <FlowStoreProvider store={store}>
      <BuilderChrome slug={slug} file={file} />
    </FlowStoreProvider>
  );
}

export function FlowBuilder({
  slug,
  file,
  initialMode,
}: {
  slug: string;
  file: Flow | null;
  initialMode: Mode;
}) {
  const [resolution, setResolution] = React.useState<Resolution>({ status: "pending" });

  React.useEffect(() => {
    // The only place this component reads localStorage. Resolving during render would make
    // this first client render disagree with the server's markup, which React reports as a
    // hydration mismatch, so the lookup waits for the mount effect instead.
    const draft = loadDraft(slug);
    const resolved = draft ?? file;
    setResolution(resolved ? { status: "resolved", flow: resolved } : { status: "missing" });
    // `slug` and `file` come from one server-rendered route; this effect is meant to run once
    // per page load, not on every re-render of its own inputs.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [slug]);

  if (resolution.status === "missing") return <FlowNotFound slug={slug} />;

  if (resolution.status === "pending") {
    // Nothing to paint yet beats a flash of empty chrome; when `file` exists, paint it right
    // away so this render matches the server's exactly.
    if (!file) return null;
    return <BuilderShell key="pending" slug={slug} initial={file} file={file} initialMode={initialMode} />;
  }

  // A different `key` than the "pending" branch forces a fresh mount, and so a fresh `useState`
  // initializer, the moment resolution completes, so a draft found after mount always replaces
  // whatever the "pending" branch rendered, including nothing at all when `file` was null.
  return (
    <BuilderShell key="resolved" slug={slug} initial={resolution.flow} file={file} initialMode={initialMode} />
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the index**

```tsx
// components/builder/flow-index.tsx
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { newDraftFlow, type Flow } from "@/lib/flow-schema";
import { listDrafts, saveDraft } from "@/lib/storage";

function formatDate(iso: string): string {
  // ISO 8601 timestamps already start with YYYY-MM-DD; slicing needs no timezone-sensitive
  // Date parsing.
  return iso.slice(0, 10);
}

function FlowCard({ flow }: { flow: Flow }) {
  return (
    <Link
      href={`/flow/${flow.id}`}
      data-slot="flow-card"
      className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-4 shadow-sm transition-shadow hover:shadow-md"
    >
      <div className="text-sm font-medium">{flow.name}</div>
      {flow.description && <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{flow.description}</div>}
      <div className="mt-auto flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-muted-foreground">
        <span>{flow.nodes.length} nodes</span>
        <span>{formatDate(flow.updatedAt)}</span>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

export function FlowIndex({ flows }: { flows: Flow[] }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [drafts, setDrafts] = React.useState<Flow[]>([]);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setDrafts(listDrafts());
  }, []);

  const createFlow = () => {
    const flow = newDraftFlow();
    saveDraft(flow);
    router.push("/flow/" + flow.id);
  };

  return (
    <div data-slot="flow-index" className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl flex-col gap-8 p-8">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Flows</h1>
        <Button type="button" onClick={createFlow}>
          New flow
        </Button>
      </div>
      <section data-slot="flow-grid" className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {flows.map((flow) => (
          <FlowCard key={flow.id} flow={flow} />
        ))}
      </section>
      <section data-slot="drafts-section" className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">Drafts in this browser</h2>
        {drafts.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">No drafts yet</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {drafts.map((flow) => (
              <FlowCard key={flow.id} flow={flow} />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### Task 11d: Server pages

**Files:**
- Create: `app/page.tsx`, `app/flow/[slug]/page.tsx`
- Test: `app/page.test.tsx`, `app/flow/[slug]/page.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  ```ts
  // lib/flows.server.ts (server only)
  export const FLOWS_DIR: string;
  export async function listFlows(dir?: string): Promise<Flow[]>;
  export async function getFlow(slug: string, dir?: string): Promise<Flow | null>;
  ```
  plus `FlowIndex(props: { flows: Flow[] })` (Task 11c) and `FlowBuilder(props: { slug: string; file: Flow | null; initialMode: Mode })` (Task 11b).
- Produces: the two routes `/` and `/flow/[slug]` the rest of the app links to (`FlowIndex`'s cards, `TopBar`'s back link, `FlowCanvas`'s `?mode=view` share link all already point at these paths). No new exported names beyond the page components and Next's route-level exports (`dynamicParams`, `generateStaticParams`, `generateMetadata`), which are conventionally named and not part of the skeleton's Shared interfaces.

`getFlow` returning `null` for an unrecognized slug does not 404 here: the spec's route table says the client checks `localStorage` for a matching draft before giving up, and only `FlowBuilder` (Task 11b) can see `localStorage`. Calling `notFound()` in this server component would 404 every draft-only flow before the client ever got a chance to look.

`listFlows`/`getFlow` call `node:fs/promises`' `readdir`/`readFile` directly against `flows/`, with no fallback for a missing directory, so an empty index needs the directory to exist, not merely to contain zero flows. Task 12 fills it with the three seed files; until then this task adds a placeholder so `pnpm build` has something to read.

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement app/flow/[slug]/page.tsx**

```tsx
// app/flow/[slug]/page.tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { getFlow, listFlows } from "@/lib/flows.server";
import { FlowBuilder } from "@/components/builder/flow-builder";

export const dynamicParams = true;

export async function generateStaticParams() {
  const flows = await listFlows();
  return flows.map((flow) => ({ slug: flow.id }));
}

type Params = Promise<{ slug: string }>;
type SearchParams = Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>;

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Params }): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const flow = await getFlow(slug);
  return { title: flow ? flow.name : "Flow" };
}

export default async function FlowPage({
  params,
  searchParams,
}: {
  params: Params;
  searchParams: SearchParams;
}) {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const flow = await getFlow(slug);

  // An unrecognized slug still renders: the flow may exist only as a browser draft, which only
  // the client can see. Calling notFound() here would 404 every draft-only flow.
  return <FlowBuilder slug={slug} file={flow} initialMode={sp.mode === "view" ? "view" : "edit"} />;
}
```

### Task 12: Seed flows

Three committed flows under `flows/`, one per source skill named in the spec's Seed flows table. Node `data` uses the field names Task 3's `KindData` defines; port names are Task 4's `NODE_CATALOG` ports for each kind. Every JSON object's keys are alphabetical and every file is 2-space indented with a trailing newline, matching Task 3's `canonicalFlowJson`, because Task 12d adds a test that checks each file against it byte for byte. Node ids are `${kind}-${n}`, counting from 1 per kind in the order nodes appear in the file; edge ids follow `edgeIdFor` (`${source}${port ? "@" + port : ""}->${target}`); handles are `${nodeId}:control:in` and `${nodeId}:control:out` or `:out@${port}`. Edges never carry a stored `label` - the skeleton's `BuilderEdge` derives the port label at render time from the source node's data, so a committed edge only ever has `id`, `source`, `sourceHandle`, `target`, `targetHandle`. Global Constraints and the skeleton's Shared interfaces apply as written and are not repeated here.

#### Task 12a: `ship` flow

**Files:**
- Create: `flows/ship.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `NodeKind`, `KindData`, `Flow`, `parseFlow` from `lib/flow-schema.ts` (Task 3) · the `control` handle codec from `lib/flow-handles.ts` (Task 3) · the `start`, `agent`, `if-else`, `mcp`, `end` port names and data fields from Task 4's `NODE_CATALOG`.
- Produces: `flows/ship.json`, one of the three files Task 12d's coverage test reads.

Source skill: `~/.claude/me-skills/skills/ship/SKILL.md`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `flows/ship.json`**

Ten nodes: `start` (manual trigger), an `agent` for the sanity check (skill step 1), an `if-else` on the working tree being clean versus dirty, a second `if-else` on the current branch being main (the never-push-to-main guardrail lives in this node's `description`, skill step 2), an `agent` for the project-brain refresh (step 3), an `agent` for the commit (step 4), three `mcp` nodes for the `git` push, the `gh` PR, and the `vercel` preview deploy (steps 5 to 7), and `end` returning the deploy link (step 8). The clean branch and the on-main branch both short-circuit straight to `end`.

```json
{
  "createdAt": "2026-09-12T00:00:00.000Z",
  "description": "Commit, push, open or update the PR, deploy a preview, and return the link.",
  "edges": [
    {
      "id": "start-1->agent-1",
      "source": "start-1",
      "sourceHandle": "start-1:control:out",
      "target": "agent-1",
      "targetHandle": "agent-1:control:in"
    },
    {
      "id": "agent-1->if-else-1",
      "source": "agent-1",
      "sourceHandle": "agent-1:control:out",
      "target": "if-else-1",
      "targetHandle": "if-else-1:control:in"
    },
    {
      "id": "if-else-1@c0->end-1",
      "source": "if-else-1",
      "sourceHandle": "if-else-1:control:out@c0",
      "target": "end-1",
      "targetHandle": "end-1:control:in"
    },
    {
      "id": "if-else-1@else->if-else-2",
      "source": "if-else-1",
      "sourceHandle": "if-else-1:control:out@else",
      "target": "if-else-2",
      "targetHandle": "if-else-2:control:in"
    },
    {
      "id": "if-else-2@c0->end-1",
      "source": "if-else-2",
      "sourceHandle": "if-else-2:control:out@c0",
      "target": "end-1",
      "targetHandle": "end-1:control:in"
    },
    {
      "id": "if-else-2@else->agent-2",
      "source": "if-else-2",
      "sourceHandle": "if-else-2:control:out@else",
      "target": "agent-2",
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `NodeKind`, `KindData`, `Flow`, `parseFlow` from `lib/flow-schema.ts` (Task 3) · the `classify`, `file-search`, `agent`, `set-state`, `user-approval`, `note`, `start`, `end` port names and data fields from Task 4's `NODE_CATALOG`.
- Produces: `flows/new-project.json`, one of the three files Task 12d's coverage test reads.

Source skill: `~/.claude/me-skills/skills/new-project/SKILL.md`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `flows/new-project.json`**

Thirteen nodes: `start`, a `classify` on the skill's three modes (personal exploration, client engagement, demo to engagement promotion), an `agent` for the light path (personal exploration exits straight to `end`, skill step 2), an `agent` for the promotion checklist that both the client-engagement and demo-to-engagement categories share (step 3), an `agent` for the identity map (step 4) feeding a `set-state` that records its three fields, a `note` carrying the guardrails list, two `file-search` nodes for the two canonical sources (the design-practice-kit template, then the local PLAYBOOK), an `agent` for seeding the HQ (step 5), an `agent` for declaring the pipeline (step 6), a `user-approval` for the kickoff scope commitments (step 7), and `end`. Both branches of the `user-approval` lead to `end`.

#### Task 12c: `subagent-build-loop` flow

**Files:**
- Create: `flows/subagent-build-loop.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `NodeKind`, `KindData`, `Flow`, `parseFlow` from `lib/flow-schema.ts` (Task 3) · the `while`, `agent`, `if-else`, `transform`, `guardrails`, `user-approval`, `start`, `end` port names and data fields from Task 4's `NODE_CATALOG`, including the rule that a `while` node's children carry `parentId` and positions relative to the frame, and that an edge may only connect two nodes sharing the same `parentId`.
- Produces: `flows/subagent-build-loop.json`, the last of the three files Task 12d's coverage test reads.

Source skill: `~/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/superpowers/6.3.0/skills/subagent-driven-development/SKILL.md`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `flows/subagent-build-loop.json`**

Ten nodes: `start`, then a `while` frame ("more tasks remain in the plan") sized to hold its five children in a row - an `agent` for the implementer (with the no-subagents contract in its instructions), an `agent` for the spec reviewer, an `agent` for the quality reviewer, an `if-else` on whether the review passed, and a `transform` that carries the ledger context to the next implementer's dispatch, fed by both the approved and the findings-open branch. After the frame, top level again: a `guardrails` node for the four stop conditions from the skill's Setup section, a `user-approval` before finishing the branch, and `end`. The frame's own `width`/`height` and its children's positions are explicit per the spec's `while` rule; the children's `x` steps by 320 starting at 32, `y` is 64 for all five.

```json

  "createdAt": "2026-09-12T00:00:00.000Z",
  "description": "Loop an implementer and reviewers over the remaining tasks until the branch is ready to finish.",

  "id": "subagent-build-loop",
  "name": "Subagent Build Loop",
  "nodes": [
    {
      "data": {
        "label": "Start",
        "trigger": "manual"
      },
      "id": "start-1",
      "kind": "start",
      "position": {
        "x": 0,
        "y": 0
      }
    },
    {
      "data": {
        "condition": "more tasks remain in the plan",
        "label": "Tasks remain"
      },
      "height": 240,
      "id": "while-1",
      "kind": "while",
      "position": {
        "x": 320,
        "y": 0
      },
      "width": 1680
    },
    {
      "data": {
        "instructions": "Implement the task: write the code, run the tests, self-review, then commit. Never dispatch subagents of your own; no helpers and no reviewers.",
        "label": "Implementer"
      },
      "id": "agent-1",
      "kind": "agent",
      "parentId": "while-1",
      "position": {
        "x": 32,
        "y": 64
      }
    },
    {
      "data": {
        "instructions": "Read the task brief and the diff. Verdict: spec compliant or not, against every requirement in the brief.",
        "label": "Spec reviewer"
      },
      "id": "agent-2",
      "kind": "agent",
      "parentId": "while-1",
      "position": {
        "x": 352,
        "y": 64
      }
    },
    {
      "data": {
        "instructions": "Read the same diff for code quality: clarity, tests, and consistency with the codebase. Verdict: approved, or findings rated Critical, Important, or Minor.",
        "label": "Quality reviewer"

      "id": "agent-3",
      "kind": "agent",
      "parentId": "while-1",
      "position": {
        "x": 672,
        "y": 64
      }

      "parentId": "while-1",
      "position": {
        "x": 992,
        "y": 64
      }
    },

      "kind": "transform",
      "parentId": "while-1",
      "position": {
        "x": 1312,

      }
    },

      "kind": "user-approval",
      "position": {
        "x": 2368,
        "y": 0
      }
    },
    {
      "data": {
```

#### Task 12d: Coverage test and `flows:format` script

**Files:**
- Create: `flows/flows.test.ts`, `scripts/format-flows.mts`
- Modify: `package.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `NODE_KINDS`, `NodeKind`, `parseFlow` from `lib/flow-schema.ts` (Task 3) · `canonicalFlowJson` from `lib/flow-convert.ts` (Task 3) · the three files Tasks 12a-12c committed.
- Produces: a regression test guarding the three seed flows, and a `pnpm flows:format` command later tasks and any future hand edit to a flow file run before committing.

Tasks 12a-12c already wrote schema-valid, canonical JSON, so this test is a regression guard rather than a red-green cycle: it is expected to pass the moment it is written, and its job is to catch the day a hand edit to one of the three files drifts from the schema, drops a kind, or breaks canonical formatting.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `flows/flows.test.ts`**

```ts
// flows/flows.test.ts
// @vitest-environment node
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { canonicalFlowJson } from "@/lib/flow-convert";
import { NODE_KINDS, parseFlow, type NodeKind } from "@/lib/flow-schema";

const SEED_SLUGS = ["ship", "new-project", "subagent-build-loop"] as const;

function readSeed(slug: string): string {
  return readFileSync(path.join(process.cwd(), "flows", `${slug}.json`), "utf8");
}

describe("seed flows", () => {
  it("every seed flow parses", () => {
    for (const slug of SEED_SLUGS) {
      const result = parseFlow(JSON.parse(readSeed(slug)));
      if (!result.ok) throw new Error(`${slug}.json: ${result.errors.join("; ")}`);
      expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("the union of kinds across the seed flows equals NODE_KINDS", () => {
    const kinds = new Set<NodeKind>();
    for (const slug of SEED_SLUGS) {
      const result = parseFlow(JSON.parse(readSeed(slug)));
      if (!result.ok) throw new Error(`${slug}.json failed to parse`);
      for (const node of result.flow.nodes) kinds.add(node.kind);
    }
    expect(kinds).toEqual(new Set(NODE_KINDS));
  });

  it.each(SEED_SLUGS)("%s.json is already canonical", (slug) => {
    const raw = readSeed(slug);
    const result = parseFlow(JSON.parse(raw));
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error(`${slug}.json failed to parse`);
    expect(raw).toBe(canonicalFlowJson(result.flow));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it**

Run: `pnpm test -- flows/flows.test.ts`
Expected: `Test Files  1 passed (1)`, `Tests  5 passed (5)` (1 parse check, 1 coverage check, 3 from `it.each` over the three slugs) - PASS on the first run, confirming Tasks 12a-12c's output rather than driving it.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `scripts/format-flows.mts`**

```ts
// scripts/format-flows.mts
// Rewrites every flows/*.json file through canonicalFlowJson, so committed
// seeds and any later hand edits always match what flows/flows.test.ts checks.
// Run through tsx (a Task 1 dev dependency), NOT bare node. Node 24 strips
// types but resolves modules like plain ESM, and lib/flow-schema.ts imports
// parseHandleId from "@/components/flow-kit/flow-types" at runtime. That "@/"
// alias is a tsconfig paths mapping, which only tsx, vitest and the Next
// bundler resolve; bare node fails with ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND.
import { readdir, readFile, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { canonicalFlowJson } from "../lib/flow-convert.ts";
import { parseFlow } from "../lib/flow-schema.ts";

const FLOWS_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), "flows");

async function main() {
  const entries = await readdir(FLOWS_DIR);
  const files = entries.filter((f) => f.endsWith(".json")).sort();
  let changed = 0;
  for (const file of files) {
    const full = path.join(FLOWS_DIR, file);
    const raw = await readFile(full, "utf8");
    const result = parseFlow(JSON.parse(raw));
    if (!result.ok) {
      throw new Error(`${file}: ${result.errors.join("; ")}`);
    }
    const canonical = canonicalFlowJson(result.flow);
    if (canonical !== raw) {
      await writeFile(full, canonical);
      changed++;
      console.log(`formatted ${file}`);
    }
  }
  console.log(`flows:format: ${files.length} file(s) checked, ${changed} rewritten.`);
}

main().catch((err) => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exitCode = 1;
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the `flows:format` package script**

Modify `package.json`'s `"scripts"` block (alongside Task 1's `test`, `lint`, `typecheck`, `check:tokens`, `build`, `e2e`, `start`), adding:

```json
"flows:format": "tsx scripts/format-flows.mts"
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Confirm the seed files are already canonical**

Run:

```bash
pnpm flows:format
git diff --stat flows/
```

Expected: the script logs `flows:format: 3 file(s) checked, 0 rewritten.` and `git diff --stat` prints nothing, since Tasks 12a-12c already wrote canonical JSON.

- [ ] **Step 6: Run the full verification suite**

```bash
pnpm test
pnpm typecheck
pnpm lint
pnpm build
```

Expected:

- `pnpm typecheck` - exits 0.
- `pnpm lint` - exits 0.
- `pnpm build` - exits 0, and for the first time actually statically renders `/` and `/flow/ship`, `/flow/new-project`, `/flow/subagent-build-loop` through `generateStaticParams`, now that `flows/` is populated.

### Task 13: Playwright journey and CI e2e

Deliverable: `playwright.config.ts`, `e2e/builder.spec.ts`, and a CI `e2e` job. Split in two: the config and journey spec are locally runnable and reviewable on their own (Task 13a); the CI wiring is an infrastructure change a reviewer might want revised independently (Task 13b).

#### Task 13a: Playwright config and the builder journey spec

**Files:**
- Create: `playwright.config.ts`
- Create: `e2e/builder.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: no TypeScript imports from the app; the spec drives the built app over HTTP and asserts on the Shared DOM contract slots (`node`, `node-title`, `node-palette`, `inspector`, `status-badge`, `flow-canvas` with `data-mode`) and the handle-id codec (`{nodeId}:control:in`, `{nodeId}:control:out`, `{nodeId}:control:out@{port}`) fixed in the skeleton. It also reads `flows/ship.json` directly with `node:fs` (Task 12's deliverable, already committed by the time this task runs) to get the seed's node count rather than hardcoding one.
- Produces: content for the `pnpm e2e` script (already defined in Task 1's `package.json`); consumed by Task 13b's CI job and by Task 14's cross-browser verification run.

This task has no red/green cycle in the usual sense: the app it drives is already fully built by Tasks 1 through 12, so there is no failing implementation to make pass. Its "run to verify" steps instead confirm the suite is real and green against the finished app.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write playwright.config.ts**

```ts
// playwright.config.ts
import { defineConfig, devices } from "@playwright/test";

const PORT = 3100;
const baseURL = process.env.PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL ?? `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: "./e2e",
  fullyParallel: true,
  forbidOnly: !!process.env.CI,
  retries: process.env.CI ? 2 : 0,
  reporter: [["html", { outputFolder: "playwright-report", open: "never" }]],
  use: {
    baseURL,
    trace: "on-first-retry",
  },
  projects: [
    { name: "chromium", use: { ...devices["Desktop Chrome"] } },
    { name: "webkit", use: { ...devices["Desktop Safari"] } },
  ],
  // Task 14 points this at a live Vercel preview by setting PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL. A deployed
  // preview already runs its own server, so this config must not also try to spawn one; the
  // local default keeps the skeleton's webServer block for every other run.
  ...(process.env.PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL
    ? {}
    : {
        webServer: {
          command: "pnpm start --port 3100",
          url: baseURL,
          reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI,
        },
      }),
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the journey spec**

```ts
// e2e/builder.spec.ts
import { test, expect, type Page } from "@playwright/test";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

function readSeed(slug: string): { nodes: unknown[] } {
  const raw = readFileSync(path.join(process.cwd(), "flows", `${slug}.json`), "utf8");
  return JSON.parse(raw) as { nodes: unknown[] };
}

async function openFlow(page: Page, url: string) {
  await page.addInitScript(() => window.localStorage.clear());
  await page.goto(url);
}

test.describe("agent flow builder journey", () => {
  test("renders the ship seed's nodes", async ({ page }) => {
    const seed = readSeed("ship");
    await openFlow(page, "/flow/ship");
    await expect(page.locator('[data-slot="node"]')).toHaveCount(seed.nodes.length);
  });

  test("adding an Agent from the palette increments the node count", async ({ page }) => {
    const seed = readSeed("ship");
    await openFlow(page, "/flow/ship");
    await page.locator('[data-slot="node-palette"]').getByRole("button", { name: "Add Agent" }).click();
    await expect(page.locator('[data-slot="node"]')).toHaveCount(seed.nodes.length + 1);
  });

  test("connecting the new node increments the edge count", async ({ page }) => {
    await openFlow(page, "/flow/ship");
    await page.locator('[data-slot="node-palette"]').getByRole("button", { name: "Add Agent" }).click();

    const newNodeId = await page.locator('[data-slot="node"]').last().getAttribute("data-id");
    const source = page.locator('[data-kind="start"] [data-handleid$=":control:out"]');
    const target = page.locator(`[data-handleid="${newNodeId}:control:in"]`);
    const edgesBefore = await page.locator(".react-flow__edge").count();

    const sourceBox = await source.boundingBox();
    const targetBox = await target.boundingBox();
    if (!sourceBox || !targetBox) throw new Error("handle not visible");

    // React Flow tracks pointer-move events to draw the in-progress connection line and to
    // resolve the drop target on release. A single teleport from mouse.down straight to
    // mouse.up never fires those intermediate events, so the connection silently fails to
    // register without the stepped move.
    await page.mouse.move(sourceBox.x + sourceBox.width / 2, sourceBox.y + sourceBox.height / 2);
    await page.mouse.down();
    await page.mouse.move(targetBox.x + targetBox.width / 2, targetBox.y + targetBox.height / 2, { steps: 10 });
    await page.mouse.up();

    await expect(page.locator(".react-flow__edge")).toHaveCount(edgesBefore + 1);
  });

  test("renaming the new node via the inspector updates its card title", async ({ page }) => {
    await openFlow(page, "/flow/ship");
    await page.locator('[data-slot="node-palette"]').getByRole("button", { name: "Add Agent" }).click();
    const newNode = page.locator('[data-slot="node"]').last();
    await newNode.click();

    await page.locator('[data-slot="inspector"]').getByLabel("Label").fill("Renamed via inspector");

    await expect(newNode.locator('[data-slot="node-title"]')).toHaveText("Renamed via inspector");
  });

  test("exports JSON containing the renamed node", async ({ page }) => {
    await openFlow(page, "/flow/ship");
    await page.locator('[data-slot="node-palette"]').getByRole("button", { name: "Add Agent" }).click();
    const newNode = page.locator('[data-slot="node"]').last();
    await newNode.click();
    await page.locator('[data-slot="inspector"]').getByLabel("Label").fill("Exported label");

    await page.locator('[data-slot="top-bar"]').getByRole("button", { name: "More options" }).click();
    const downloadPromise = page.waitForEvent("download");
    await page.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Export JSON" }).click();
    const download = await downloadPromise;
    const filePath = await download.path();
    if (!filePath) throw new Error("download did not save to disk");
    const exported = JSON.parse(readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")) as { nodes: { data: { label: string } }[] };

    expect(exported.nodes.some((n) => n.data.label === "Exported label")).toBe(true);
  });

  test("reload restores the added node and shows a Draft status", async ({ page }) => {
    const seed = readSeed("ship");
    await openFlow(page, "/flow/ship");
    await page.locator('[data-slot="node-palette"]').getByRole("button", { name: "Add Agent" }).click();
    await expect(page.locator('[data-slot="node"]')).toHaveCount(seed.nodes.length + 1);

    // Drafts persist 500ms after the last change (lib/draft-persistence.ts). Reloading before
    // that write lands would restore nothing, and this assertion would see the committed file
    // instead of the draft.
    await page.waitForTimeout(700);
    await page.reload();

    await expect(page.locator('[data-slot="node"]')).toHaveCount(seed.nodes.length + 1);
    await expect(page.locator('[data-slot="status-badge"]')).toHaveText("Draft");
  });

  test("?mode=view hides the palette and inspector, locks dragging, matches the node count", async ({ page }) => {
    const seed = readSeed("ship");
    await openFlow(page, "/flow/ship?mode=view");

    await expect(page.locator('[data-slot="flow-canvas"]')).toHaveAttribute("data-mode", "view");
    await expect(page.locator('[data-slot="node-palette"]')).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.locator('[data-slot="inspector"]')).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(page.locator('[data-slot="node"]')).toHaveCount(seed.nodes.length);

    const nodeWrapper = page.locator(".react-flow__node").first();
    const before = await nodeWrapper.evaluate((el) => getComputedStyle(el).transform);
    const box = await nodeWrapper.boundingBox();
    if (!box) throw new Error("node not visible");
    await page.mouse.move(box.x + box.width / 2, box.y + box.height / 2);
    await page.mouse.down();
    await page.mouse.move(box.x + box.width / 2 + 120, box.y + box.height / 2 + 80, { steps: 10 });
    await page.mouse.up();
    const after = await nodeWrapper.evaluate((el) => getComputedStyle(el).transform);

    expect(after).toBe(before);
  });
});
```

#### Task 13b: CI e2e job

**Files:**
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the `verify` job Task 1 wrote (lint, typecheck, check:tokens, test, build) and the `pnpm e2e` script Task 1 defined, now runnable thanks to Task 13a.
- Produces: a second job, `e2e`, that gates merges on the Playwright suite passing on both browsers.

- [ ] **Step 1: Append the e2e job**

Show the full file; only the `e2e` job at the bottom is new, the `verify` job is unchanged from Task 1.

```yaml
# .github/workflows/ci.yml
name: CI
on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:

jobs:
  verify:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with: { version: 11.1.0 }
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with: { node-version: 24, cache: pnpm }
      - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
      - run: pnpm lint
      - run: pnpm typecheck
      - run: pnpm check:tokens
      - run: pnpm test
      - run: pnpm build

  e2e:
    needs: verify
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with: { version: 11.1.0 }
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with: { node-version: 24, cache: pnpm }
      - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
      - run: pnpm exec playwright install --with-deps chromium webkit
      - run: pnpm build
      - run: pnpm e2e
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        if: failure()
        with:
          name: playwright-report
          path: playwright-report/
          retention-days: 7
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Confirm the file reads back as written**

```bash
cat .github/workflows/ci.yml
```

Expected: prints exactly the two jobs above. This repository has no local YAML linter in its toolchain, so the definitive syntax check is GitHub Actions itself running this file once Task 14 pushes the branch; a bad indent or key would show up there as a workflow parse failure, not here.

```bash
Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF
)"
```

### Task 14: Vercel preview and cross-browser verification

Deliverable: an unlisted Vercel preview URL for `feat/wave-1-draw`, verified in Chromium and WebKit with no console errors, README updated, PR opened. This task is an ordered runbook rather than a TDD cycle: everything it touches (the app, the e2e suite) already exists from Tasks 1 through 13, so each step is a real command against the live preview with a real expected result, not a red/green pair.

Never pass `--prod`. A plain `vercel` deploy is a preview by default.

```bash
vercel --yes | tee /tmp/vercel-deploy.log

echo "$PREVIEW_URL"
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Check the three routes return 200**

```bash
for route in "/" "/flow/ship" "/flow/ship?mode=view"; do
  code=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" "${PREVIEW_URL}${route}")
  echo "${route} -> ${code}"
done
```

Expected: all three lines show `200`. A `401` instead means Vercel's deployment protection is on for this project; turn it off (or capture a bypass token) for this preview before continuing, since an unlisted preview still needs to be reachable by anyone with the link.

```bash
PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL="$PREVIEW_URL" pnpm e2e
```

Expected: `14 passed` (Task 13's 7 journey tests on `chromium` and `webkit`). `playwright.config.ts` skips `webServer` whenever `PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL` is set, so this runs against the deployed preview, not a local server.

- [ ] **Step 6: Add and run a console-error check**

```ts
// e2e/console.spec.ts
import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";

const ROUTES = ["/", "/flow/ship", "/flow/ship?mode=view"];

for (const route of ROUTES) {
  test(`no console or page errors on ${route}`, async ({ page }) => {
    const errors: string[] = [];
    page.on("console", (msg) => {
      if (msg.type() === "error") errors.push(msg.text());
    });
    page.on("pageerror", (err) => errors.push(err.message));

    await page.addInitScript(() => window.localStorage.clear());
    await page.goto(route);
    await page.waitForLoadState("networkidle");

    expect(errors, `console/page errors on ${route}: ${errors.join("; ")}`).toEqual([]);
  });
}
```

```bash
PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL="$PREVIEW_URL" pnpm exec playwright test e2e/console.spec.ts
```

Expected: `6 passed` (3 routes on 2 browsers), zero console or page errors on any of them.

```bash
gh pr create --base main --head feat/wave-1-draw \
  --title "feat: agent flow builder wave 1 (Draw)" \
  --body "$(cat <<EOF
## Summary
Wave 1 (Draw) of the agent flow builder: a Next.js app where flows are drawn on a React Flow canvas, saved as committed JSON plus browser drafts, and shared as locked view-mode pages.

Preview: ${PREVIEW_URL}

## Against the spec's success criteria
1. \`/\` lists the three seed flows (ship, new-project, subagent-build-loop) and each renders its committed nodes, edges and notes.
2. Palette add, connect, inspector rename, export, and import round-trip covered in \`e2e/builder.spec.ts\`.
3. \`while\` frame containment and cross-boundary connection rejection covered in \`lib/group-utils.test.ts\` and \`lib/connection-rules.test.ts\`.
```
